    17	package org.apache.kafka.network.iouring;
    18	
    19	import org.slf4j.Logger;
    20	import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
    21	
    22	import java.net.InetSocketAddress;
    23	import java.util.Objects;
    24	import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;
    25	
    26	import io.netty.bootstrap.ServerBootstrap;
    27	import io.netty.buffer.ByteBuf;
    28	import io.netty.channel.Channel;
    29	import io.netty.channel.ChannelFuture;
    30	import io.netty.channel.ChannelHandlerContext;
    31	import io.netty.channel.ChannelInboundHandlerAdapter;
    32	import io.netty.channel.ChannelInitializer;
    33	import io.netty.channel.ChannelOption;
    34	import io.netty.channel.EventLoopGroup;
    35	import io.netty.channel.MultiThreadIoEventLoopGroup;
    36	import io.netty.channel.unix.UnixChannelOption;
    37	import io.netty.channel.uring.IoUringIoHandler;
    38	import io.netty.channel.uring.IoUringServerSocketChannel;
    39	import io.netty.util.ReferenceCountUtil;
    40	
    41	/**
    42	 * Binds an {@code IoUringServerSocketChannel} on the listener address and wires every
    43	 * accepted child channel into an {@link IoUringSelector}.
    44	 *
    45	 * <h3>What this class owns</h3>
    46	 * <ul>
    47	 *   <li>A single-thread Netty {@code MultiThreadIoEventLoopGroup} backed by
    48	 *       {@code IoUringIoHandler} — the kernel-side ring driver.</li>
    49	 *   <li>The bound {@link IoUringServerSocketChannel} on the listener port. The
    50	 *       {@link UnixChannelOption#SO_REUSEPORT} flag is set so multiple per-Processor
    51	 *       listeners can share the same operator-configured port — the kernel does the
    52	 *       accept-sharding.</li>
    53	 *   <li>A child-channel pipeline that pushes every event back into the supplied
    54	 *       {@link IoUringSelector} via its four package-private hooks.</li>
    55	 * </ul>
    56	 *
    57	 * <h3>What this class does NOT own</h3>
    58	 * The {@link IoUringSelector} — the Processor that owns the selector also owns this
    59	 * listener. {@link #close()} closes the server socket and shuts the event loop down, but
    60	 * does <em>not</em> close the selector; that lifecycle stays with the Processor.
    61	 *
    62	 * <h3>Thread safety</h3>
    63	 * Construction binds synchronously and may block briefly. {@link #close()} is
    64	 * idempotent and safe to call from any thread; subsequent calls return immediately.
    65	 * The internal Netty handler runs entirely on the event-loop thread and is the only
    66	 * thread that touches the selector's event-loop callbacks.
    67	 */
    68	public final class IoUringServerListener implements AutoCloseable {
    69	
    70	    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(IoUringServerListener.class);
    71	
    72	    /** Netty's quiet-period for shutdownGracefully — kept short so broker shutdown isn't slow. */
    73	    private static final long SHUTDOWN_QUIET_MS = 100;
    74	    /** Netty's overall shutdown deadline. */
    75	    private static final long SHUTDOWN_TIMEOUT_MS = 5_000;
    76	
    77	    private final EventLoopGroup eventLoopGroup;
    78	    private final Channel serverChannel;
    79	    private final IoUringSelector selector;
    80	    private final int boundPort;
    81	    private volatile boolean closed;
    82	
    83	    /** Sentinel meaning "leave the OS default in place" — matches {@code Selectable.USE_DEFAULT_BUFFER_SIZE}. */
    84	    public static final int USE_DEFAULT_BUFFER_SIZE = -1;
    85	
    86	    public IoUringServerListener(InetSocketAddress bindAddress, IoUringSelector selector) {
    87	        this(bindAddress, selector, /*soBacklog*/ 128, USE_DEFAULT_BUFFER_SIZE, USE_DEFAULT_BUFFER_SIZE);
    88	    }
    89	
    90	    public IoUringServerListener(InetSocketAddress bindAddress, IoUringSelector selector, int soBacklog) {
    91	        this(bindAddress, selector, soBacklog, USE_DEFAULT_BUFFER_SIZE, USE_DEFAULT_BUFFER_SIZE);
    92	    }
    93	
    94	    /**
    95	     * @param sendBufferSize    {@code SO_SNDBUF} applied to every accepted child channel, or
    96	     *                          {@link #USE_DEFAULT_BUFFER_SIZE} to leave the OS default in place
    97	     *                          ({@code socket.send.buffer.bytes} on the NIO path).
    98	     * @param receiveBufferSize {@code SO_RCVBUF} applied to the listening socket so newly accepted
    99	     *                          children inherit it via standard TCP semantics, or
   100	     *                          {@link #USE_DEFAULT_BUFFER_SIZE} for the OS default
   101	     *                          ({@code socket.receive.buffer.bytes} on the NIO path).
   102	     */
   103	    public IoUringServerListener(InetSocketAddress bindAddress,
   104	                                 IoUringSelector selector,
   105	                                 int soBacklog,
   106	                                 int sendBufferSize,
   107	                                 int receiveBufferSize) {
   108	        Objects.requireNonNull(bindAddress, "bindAddress");
   109	        this.selector = Objects.requireNonNull(selector, "selector");
   110	        if (!IoUringSupport.isAvailable()) {
   111	            throw new IllegalStateException(
   112	                "io_uring is not available on this host: " + IoUringSupport.unavailabilityReason());
   113	        }
   114	
   115	        this.eventLoopGroup = new MultiThreadIoEventLoopGroup(1, IoUringIoHandler.newFactory());
   116	
   117	        try {
   118	            // Mirror NIO Acceptor.configureAcceptedSocketChannel (SocketServer.scala:740-746):
   119	            // every accepted broker connection gets TCP_NODELAY=true and SO_KEEPALIVE=true.
   120	            // TCP_NODELAY disables Nagle's algorithm — critical for Kafka request/response
   121	            // latency since requests are batched at the producer/consumer layer and Nagle
   122	            // would add hundreds of microseconds on every flush. SO_KEEPALIVE detects half-open
   123	            // connections so the broker doesn't keep a dead peer slot indefinitely.
   124	            // SO_SNDBUF is applied per-child when the operator overrides the default; SO_RCVBUF
   125	            // is applied to the LISTEN socket (children inherit via TCP) matching how NIO sets
   126	            // it on serverSocket.openServerSocket(..., recvBufferSize) at SocketServer.scala:667.
   127	            ServerBootstrap bootstrap = new ServerBootstrap()
   128	                .group(eventLoopGroup)
   129	                .channel(IoUringServerSocketChannel.class)
   130	                .option(ChannelOption.SO_BACKLOG, soBacklog)
   131	                .option(UnixChannelOption.SO_REUSEPORT, true)
   132	                .childOption(ChannelOption.AUTO_READ, true)
   133	                .childOption(ChannelOption.TCP_NODELAY, true)
   134	                .childOption(ChannelOption.SO_KEEPALIVE, true)
   135	                .childHandler(new ChannelInitializer<Channel>() {
   136	                    @Override
   137	                    protected void initChannel(Channel ch) {
   138	                        ch.pipeline().addLast(new ChildHandler(selector));
   139	                    }
   140	                });
   141	            if (sendBufferSize != USE_DEFAULT_BUFFER_SIZE) {
   142	                bootstrap.childOption(ChannelOption.SO_SNDBUF, sendBufferSize);
   143	            }
   144	            if (receiveBufferSize != USE_DEFAULT_BUFFER_SIZE) {
   145	                // SO_RCVBUF on the LISTEN socket: TCP semantics propagate it to every accepted
   146	                // child at the moment of accept. Setting it per-child would be ineffective
   147	                // because the OS sizes the receive buffer at accept time, not afterwards.
   148	                bootstrap.option(ChannelOption.SO_RCVBUF, receiveBufferSize);
   149	            }
   150	
   151	            ChannelFuture future = bootstrap.bind(bindAddress).sync();
   152	            this.serverChannel = future.channel();
   153	            this.boundPort = ((InetSocketAddress) serverChannel.localAddress()).getPort();
   154	            log.info("io_uring listener bound to {} (port {}, sendBufferSize={}, receiveBufferSize={})",
   155	                bindAddress, boundPort, sendBufferSize, receiveBufferSize);
   156	        } catch (InterruptedException ie) {
   157	            eventLoopGroup.shutdownGracefully();
   158	            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
   159	            throw new IllegalStateException("Interrupted while binding io_uring listener", ie);
   160	        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
   161	            eventLoopGroup.shutdownGracefully();
   162	            throw e;
   163	        }
   164	    }
   165	
   166	    /** Returns the actually bound port — useful when the caller passed port 0. */
   167	    public int boundPort() {
   168	        return boundPort;
   169	    }
   170	
   171	    /** The selector that channels are wired to. Returned for symmetry; the caller already has it. */
   172	    public IoUringSelector selector() {
   173	        return selector;
   174	    }
   175	
   176	    @Override
   177	    public void close() {
   178	        if (closed) return;
   179	        closed = true;
   180	        try {
   181	            serverChannel.close().sync();
   182	        } catch (InterruptedException ie) {
   183	            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
   184	        } catch (Exception e) {
   185	            log.debug("error closing io_uring server channel", e);
   186	        }
   187	        eventLoopGroup.shutdownGracefully(SHUTDOWN_QUIET_MS, SHUTDOWN_TIMEOUT_MS, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS)
   188	            .syncUninterruptibly();
   189	    }
   190	
   191	    /**
   192	     * The per-child-channel Netty handler. One instance is added to every accepted
   193	     * channel's pipeline and forwards each event-loop event to the corresponding
   194	     * package-private hook on {@link IoUringSelector}.
   195	     */
   196	    private static final class ChildHandler extends ChannelInboundHandlerAdapter {
   197	
   198	        private final IoUringSelector selector;
   199	
   200	        ChildHandler(IoUringSelector selector) {
   201	            this.selector = selector;
   202	        }
   203	
   204	        @Override
   205	        public void channelActive(ChannelHandlerContext ctx) {
   206	            InetSocketAddress remote = (InetSocketAddress) ctx.channel().remoteAddress();
   207	            InetSocketAddress local = (InetSocketAddress) ctx.channel().localAddress();
   208	            selector.onAccept(ctx.channel(), remote, local);
   209	        }
   210	
   211	        @Override
   212	        public void channelRead(ChannelHandlerContext ctx, Object msg) {
   213	            if (msg instanceof ByteBuf) {
   214	                selector.onRead(ctx.channel(), (ByteBuf) msg);
   215	            } else {
   216	                // io_uring always delivers ByteBuf for inbound bytes; anything else is unexpected.
   217	                ReferenceCountUtil.release(msg);
   218	            }
   219	        }
   220	
   221	        @Override
   222	        public void channelInactive(ChannelHandlerContext ctx) {
   223	            selector.onDisconnect(ctx.channel());
   224	        }
   225	
   226	        @Override
   227	        public void channelWritabilityChanged(ChannelHandlerContext ctx) {
   228	            selector.onWritabilityChanged(ctx.channel());
   229	        }
   230	
   231	        @Override
   232	        public void exceptionCaught(ChannelHandlerContext ctx, Throwable cause) {
   233	            log.debug("io_uring child channel raised an exception; closing", cause);
   234	            ctx.close();
   235	        }
   236	    }
   237	}

    33	/**
    34	 * The PLAINTEXT {@link Authenticator} the io_uring backend wraps around every accepted
    35	 * channel.
    36	 *
    37	 * <p>Mirrors the private {@code PlaintextChannelBuilder.PlaintextAuthenticator} in
    38	 * {@code clients/} — that class is package-private and cannot be reused from {@code server/}.
    39	 * The behaviour is identical:
    40	 *
    41	 * <ul>
    42	 *   <li>{@link #authenticate()} is a no-op (PLAINTEXT has no handshake).</li>
    43	 *   <li>{@link #complete()} is always true.</li>
    44	 *   <li>{@link #principal()} reads the remote {@link InetAddress} off the transport layer
    45	 *       and calls the configured {@link KafkaPrincipalBuilder}. With the default builder
    46	 *       this returns {@link KafkaPrincipal#ANONYMOUS}.</li>
    47	 * </ul>
    48	 */
    49	final class IoUringPlaintextAuthenticator implements Authenticator {
    50	
    51	    private final IoUringTransportLayer transportLayer;
    52	    private final KafkaPrincipalBuilder principalBuilder;
    53	    private final ListenerName listenerName;
    54	
    55	    IoUringPlaintextAuthenticator(IoUringTransportLayer transportLayer, ListenerName listenerName,
    56	                                  Map<String, ?> configs) {
    57	        this.transportLayer = transportLayer;
    58	        this.listenerName = listenerName;
    59	        // Match PlaintextChannelBuilder.PlaintextAuthenticator: the broker's parsed configs
    60	        // are required so a user-configured PRINCIPAL_BUILDER_CLASS_CONFIG is honored. Passing
    61	        // an empty map silently falls back to DefaultKafkaPrincipalBuilder, which is a
    62	        // semantic divergence from the NIO path on the same broker.
    63	        this.principalBuilder = ChannelBuilders.createPrincipalBuilder(configs, null, null);
    64	    }
    65	
    66	    @Override
    67	    public void authenticate() {
    68	        // PLAINTEXT: no handshake.
    69	    }
    70	
    71	    @Override
    72	    public KafkaPrincipal principal() {
    73	        InetAddress clientAddress = transportLayer.socketChannel().socket().getInetAddress();
    74	        return principalBuilder.build(new PlaintextAuthenticationContext(clientAddress, listenerName.value()));
    75	    }
    76	
    77	    @Override
    78	    public Optional<KafkaPrincipalSerde> principalSerde() {
    79	        return principalBuilder instanceof KafkaPrincipalSerde
    80	            ? Optional.of((KafkaPrincipalSerde) principalBuilder)
    81	            : Optional.empty();
    82	    }
    83	
    84	    @Override
    85	    public boolean complete() {
    86	        return true;
    87	    }
    88	
    89	    @Override
    90	    public void close() {
    91	        if (principalBuilder instanceof Closeable) {
    92	            Utils.closeQuietly((Closeable) principalBuilder, "principal builder");
    93	        }
    94	    }
    95	}

   177	package org.apache.kafka.network.iouring;
   178	
   179	import org.apache.kafka.common.security.auth.SecurityProtocol;
   180	import org.apache.kafka.network.SocketServerConfigs;
   181	
   182	/**
   183	 * Decision function that maps the operator-requested
   184	 * {@link SelectorImplementation}, the listener's {@link SecurityProtocol}, and a
   185	 * platform availability flag to the effective implementation a per-listener
   186	 * Processor should construct.
   187	 *
   188	 * <p>Rules (v1):
   189	 * <ul>
   190	 *   <li>Explicit {@code nio} always wins.</li>
   191	 *   <li>io_uring is gated on PLAINTEXT only; SSL / SASL_PLAINTEXT / SASL_SSL transparently
   192	 *       fall back to NIO regardless of the request.</li>
   193	 *   <li>{@code auto} resolves to io_uring iff the platform supports it; otherwise NIO.</li>
   194	 *   <li>Explicit {@code io_uring} on a PLAINTEXT listener with no platform support is a
   195	 *       hard error — the operator asked for io_uring; silently downgrading would hide
   196	 *       configuration drift.</li>
   197	 * </ul>
   198	 */
   199	public final class BrokerSelectorFactory {
   200	
   201	    private BrokerSelectorFactory() { }
   202	
   203	    /**
   204	     * @param requested  operator's parsed {@link SelectorImplementation} config value
   205	     * @param protocol   the listener's security protocol
   206	     * @param ioUringAvailable platform probe result — typically {@link IoUringSupport#isAvailable()}
   207	     * @return the effective implementation to construct
   208	     * @throws IllegalStateException when {@code io_uring} is explicitly requested for a
   209	     *         PLAINTEXT listener but the host has no io_uring support
   210	     */
   211	    public static SelectorImplementation resolve(SelectorImplementation requested,
   212	                                                 SecurityProtocol protocol,
   213	                                                 boolean ioUringAvailable) {
   214	        if (requested == SelectorImplementation.NIO) {
   215	            return SelectorImplementation.NIO;
   216	        }
   217	        // v1 PLAINTEXT-only constraint: any non-PLAINTEXT listener transparently uses NIO.
   218	        if (protocol != SecurityProtocol.PLAINTEXT) {
   219	            return SelectorImplementation.NIO;
   220	        }
   221	        if (requested == SelectorImplementation.AUTO) {
   222	            return ioUringAvailable ? SelectorImplementation.IO_URING : SelectorImplementation.NIO;
   223	        }
   224	        // Explicit IO_URING on a PLAINTEXT listener.
   225	        if (!ioUringAvailable) {
   226	            String reason = IoUringSupport.unavailabilityReason();
   227	            throw new IllegalStateException(
   228	                SocketServerConfigs.SOCKET_SELECTOR_IMPLEMENTATION_CONFIG
   229	                    + "=" + SelectorImplementation.IO_URING.configValue()
   230	                    + " was requested but io_uring is unavailable on this host: "
   231	                    + (reason != null ? reason : "unknown reason"));
   232	        }
   233	        return SelectorImplementation.IO_URING;
   234	    }
   235	}
   236	/*
   237	 * Licensed to the Apache Software Foundation (ASF) under one or more
   238	 * contributor license agreements. See the NOTICE file distributed with
   239	 * this work for additional information regarding copyright ownership.
   240	 * The ASF licenses this file to You under the Apache License, Version 2.0
   241	 * (the "License"); you may not use this file except in compliance with
   242	 * the License. You may obtain a copy of the License at

   252	package org.apache.kafka.network.iouring;
   253	
   254	import org.slf4j.Logger;
   255	import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
   256	
   257	import java.lang.reflect.Method;
   258	import java.util.Locale;
   259	
   260	/**
   261	 * Runtime probe for whether the Netty io_uring transport is usable on this JVM/host.
   262	 *
   263	 * <p>The probe is reflective so this class itself loads on any platform — including
   264	 * macOS / Windows / Linux JVMs that don't ship the io_uring native library — without
   265	 * triggering a NoClassDefFoundError.
   266	 *
   267	 * <p>The result is cached; the underlying conditions (OS, kernel, native lib) don't
   268	 * change during a JVM run.
   269	 */
   270	public final class IoUringSupport {
   271	
   272	    private static final Logger LOG = LoggerFactory.getLogger(IoUringSupport.class);
   273	    private static final String IO_URING_PROBE_CLASS = "io.netty.channel.uring.IoUring";
   274	
   275	    private static final boolean LINUX =
   276	        System.getProperty("os.name", "").toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).contains("linux");
   277	
   278	    private static final Probe PROBE = computeProbe();
   279	
   280	    private IoUringSupport() { }
   281	
   282	    /** @return true iff this JVM is running on Linux. */
   283	    public static boolean isLinux() {
   284	        return LINUX;
   285	    }
   286	
   287	    /**
   288	     * @return true iff the Netty io_uring transport reports itself usable on this host
   289	     *         (Linux kernel with io_uring support and the native lib loadable).
   290	     */
   291	    public static boolean isAvailable() {
   292	        return PROBE.available;
   293	    }
   294	
   295	    /**
   296	     * @return a human-readable reason why io_uring is unavailable, or {@code null} when
   297	     *         {@link #isAvailable()} is {@code true}. The reason is suitable for inclusion
   298	     *         in startup logs and error messages.
   299	     */
   300	    public static String unavailabilityReason() {
   301	        return PROBE.reason;
   302	    }
   303	
   304	    private static Probe computeProbe() {
   305	        if (!LINUX) {
   306	            return new Probe(false, "io_uring requires Linux (this JVM reports os.name="
   307	                + System.getProperty("os.name") + ")");
   308	        }
   309	        try {
   310	            Class<?> ioUring = Class.forName(IO_URING_PROBE_CLASS, true, IoUringSupport.class.getClassLoader());
   311	            Method isAvailable = ioUring.getMethod("isAvailable");
   312	            boolean available = (Boolean) isAvailable.invoke(null);
   313	            if (available) {
   314	                return new Probe(true, null);
   315	            }
   316	            String cause = describeUnavailabilityCause(ioUring);
   317	            return new Probe(false, "Netty io_uring transport reports unavailable: " + cause);
   318	        } catch (ClassNotFoundException e) {
   319	            return new Probe(false, "Netty io_uring transport classes not on the classpath ("
   320	                + IO_URING_PROBE_CLASS + ")");
   321	        } catch (ReflectiveOperationException e) {
   322	            LOG.debug("Failed to probe Netty io_uring availability via reflection", e);
   323	            return new Probe(false, "Reflective probe of " + IO_URING_PROBE_CLASS
   324	                + " failed: " + e.getClass().getSimpleName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
   325	        }
   326	    }
   327	
   328	    private static String describeUnavailabilityCause(Class<?> ioUring) {
   329	        try {
   330	            Method unavailabilityCause = ioUring.getMethod("unavailabilityCause");
   331	            Object cause = unavailabilityCause.invoke(null);
   332	            if (cause == null) return "no cause reported";
   333	            return cause.getClass().getSimpleName() + ": " + ((Throwable) cause).getMessage();
   334	        } catch (ReflectiveOperationException e) {
   335	            return "unavailabilityCause() unreadable (" + e.getClass().getSimpleName() + ")";
   336	        }
   337	    }
   338	
   339	    private static final class Probe {
   340	        final boolean available;
   341	        final String reason;
   342	        Probe(boolean available, String reason) {
   343	            this.available = available;
   344	            this.reason = reason;
   345	        }
   346	    }
   347	}

   540	    this)
   541	
   542	  def start(): Unit = synchronized {
   543	    try {
   544	      if (!shouldRun.get()) {
   545	        throw new ClosedChannelException()
   546	      }
   547	      // io_uring listeners bind via the per-Processor IoUringServerListener (SO_REUSEPORT),
   548	      // so the Acceptor must NOT open a NIO ServerSocketChannel on the same address — doing
   549	      // so would either fail with EADDRINUSE (if the Processor bound first) or steal accepts
   550	      // away from the io_uring path. The Acceptor thread still starts, but its run loop
   551	      // skips the NIO accept work for io_uring listeners.
   552	      if (serverChannel == null && !usesIoUring) {
   553	        serverChannel = openServerSocket(endPoint.host, endPoint.port, listenBacklogSize)
   554	        debug(s"Opened endpoint ${endPoint.host}:${endPoint.port}")
   555	      }
   556	      debug(s"Starting processors for listener ${endPoint.listenerName}")
   557	      processors.foreach(_.start())
   558	      debug(s"Starting acceptor thread for listener ${endPoint.listenerName}")
   559	      thread.start()
   560	      startedFuture.complete(null)
   561	      started.set(true)
   562	    } catch {
   563	      case e: ClosedChannelException =>
   564	        debug(s"Refusing to start acceptor for ${endPoint.listenerName} since the acceptor has already been shut down.")
   565	        startedFuture.completeExceptionally(e)
   566	      case t: Throwable =>
   567	        error(s"Unable to start acceptor for ${endPoint.listenerName}", t)
   568	        startedFuture.completeExceptionally(new RuntimeException(s"Unable to start acceptor for ${endPoint.listenerName}", t))
   569	    }
   570	  }
   571	
   572	  private[network] case class DelayedCloseSocket(socket: SocketChannel, endThrottleTimeMs: Long) extends Ordered[DelayedCloseSocket] {
   573	    override def compare(that: DelayedCloseSocket): Int = endThrottleTimeMs compare that.endThrottleTimeMs
   574	  }
   575	
   576	  private[network] def removeProcessors(removeCount: Int): Unit = synchronized {
   577	    // Shutdown `removeCount` processors. Remove them from the processor list first so that no more
   578	    // connections are assigned. Shutdown the removed processors, closing the selector and its connections.
   579	    // The processors are then removed from `requestChannel` and any pending responses to these processors are dropped.
   580	    val toRemove = processors.takeRight(removeCount)
   581	    processors.remove(processors.size - removeCount, removeCount)
   582	    toRemove.foreach(_.close())
   583	    toRemove.foreach(processor => requestChannel.removeProcessor(processor.id))
   584	  }
   585	
   586	  def beginShutdown(): Unit = {
   587	    if (shouldRun.getAndSet(false)) {
   588	      wakeup()
   589	      synchronized {
   590	        processors.foreach(_.beginShutdown())
   591	      }
   592	    }
   593	  }
   594	
   595	  def close(): Unit = {
   596	    beginShutdown()
   597	    thread.join()
   598	    if (!started.get) {
   599	      closeAll()
   600	    }
   601	    synchronized {
   602	      processors.foreach(_.close())
   603	    }
   604	  }
   605	
   606	  /**
   607	   * Accept loop that checks for new connection attempts. For io_uring listeners the per-
   608	   * Processor IoUringServerListener owns the accept loop via Netty, so the Acceptor thread
   609	   * has nothing to dispatch — it just blocks on the nioSelector with a timeout so wakeup()
   610	   * during shutdown still returns promptly.
   611	   */
   612	  override def run(): Unit = {
   613	    val ioUring = usesIoUring
   614	    if (!ioUring) {
   615	      serverChannel.register(nioSelector, SelectionKey.OP_ACCEPT)
   616	    }
   617	    try {
   618	      while (shouldRun.get()) {
   619	        try {
   620	          if (ioUring) {
   621	            nioSelector.select(500)
   622	            nioSelector.selectedKeys().clear()
   623	          } else {
   624	            acceptNewConnections()
   625	          }
   626	          closeThrottledConnections()
   627	        }
   628	        catch {
   629	          // We catch all the throwables to prevent the acceptor thread from exiting on exceptions due
   630	          // to a select operation on a specific channel or a bad request. We don't want
   631	          // the broker to stop responding to requests from other clients in these scenarios.
   632	          case e: ControlThrowable => throw e
   633	          case e: Throwable => error("Error occurred", e)
   634	        }
   635	      }
   636	    } finally {
   637	      closeAll()
   638	    }
   639	  }
   640	
   641	  /** Whether this Acceptor's listener is served by the io_uring backend. */
   642	  private[network] def usesIoUring: Boolean =
   643	    config.usesIoUring(endPoint.securityProtocol)
   644	
   645	  private def closeAll(): Unit = {
   646	    debug("Closing server socket, selector, and any throttled sockets.")
   647	    // The serverChannel will be null if Acceptor's thread is not started
   648	    Utils.closeQuietly(serverChannel, "Acceptor serverChannel")
   649	    Utils.closeQuietly(nioSelector, "Acceptor nioSelector")
   650	    throttledSockets.foreach(throttledSocket => closeSocket(throttledSocket.socket))
   651	    throttledSockets.clear()
   652	  }
   653	
   654	  /**
   655	   * Create a server socket to listen for connections on.
   656	   */
   657	  private def openServerSocket(host: String, port: Int, listenBacklogSize: Int): ServerSocketChannel = {
   658	    val socketAddress = if (Utils.isBlank(host)) {
   659	      new InetSocketAddress(port)
   660	    } else {
   661	      new InetSocketAddress(host, port)
   662	    }
   663	    val serverChannel = socketServer.socketFactory.openServerSocket(
   664	      endPoint.listenerName.value(),
   665	      socketAddress,
   666	      listenBacklogSize,
   667	      recvBufferSize)
   668	    info(s"Awaiting socket connections on ${socketAddress.getHostString}:${serverChannel.socket.getLocalPort}.")
   669	    serverChannel
   670	  }
   671	
   672	  /**
   673	   * Listen for new connections and assign accepted connections to processors using round-robin.
   674	   */
   675	  private def acceptNewConnections(): Unit = {
   676	    val ready = nioSelector.select(500)
   677	    if (ready > 0) {
   678	      val keys = nioSelector.selectedKeys()
   679	      val iter = keys.iterator()
   680	      while (iter.hasNext && shouldRun.get()) {
   681	        try {
   682	          val key = iter.next
   683	          iter.remove()
   684	
   685	          if (key.isAcceptable) {
   686	            accept(key).foreach { socketChannel =>
   687	              // Assign the channel to the next processor (using round-robin) to which the
   688	              // channel can be added without blocking. If newConnections queue is full on
   689	              // all processors, block until the last one is able to accept a connection.
   690	              var retriesLeft = synchronized(processors.length)
   691	              var processor: Processor = null
   692	              do {
   693	                retriesLeft -= 1
   694	                processor = synchronized {
   695	                  // adjust the index (if necessary) and retrieve the processor atomically for
   696	                  // correct behaviour in case the number of processors is reduced dynamically
   697	                  currentProcessorIndex = currentProcessorIndex % processors.length
   698	                  processors(currentProcessorIndex)
   699	                }
   700	                currentProcessorIndex += 1
   701	              } while (!assignNewConnection(socketChannel, processor, retriesLeft == 0))
   702	            }
   703	          } else
   704	            throw new IllegalStateException("Unrecognized key state for acceptor thread.")
   705	        } catch {
   706	          case e: Throwable => error("Error while accepting connection", e)
   707	        }
   708	      }
   709	    }
   710	  }
   711	
   712	  /**
   713	   * Accept a new connection
   714	   */
   715	  private def accept(key: SelectionKey): Option[SocketChannel] = {
   716	    val serverSocketChannel = key.channel().asInstanceOf[ServerSocketChannel]
   717	    val socketChannel = serverSocketChannel.accept()
   718	    try {
   719	      connectionQuotas.inc(endPoint.listenerName, socketChannel.socket.getInetAddress, blockedPercentMeter)
   720	      configureAcceptedSocketChannel(socketChannel)
   721	      Some(socketChannel)
   722	    } catch {
   723	      case e: TooManyConnectionsException =>
   724	        info(s"Rejected connection from ${e.ip}, address already has the configured maximum of ${e.count} connections.")
   725	        connectionQuotas.closeChannel(this, endPoint.listenerName, socketChannel)
   726	        None
   727	      case e: ConnectionThrottledException =>
   728	        val ip = socketChannel.socket.getInetAddress
   729	        debug(s"Delaying closing of connection from $ip for ${e.throttleTimeMs} ms")
   730	        val endThrottleTimeMs = e.startThrottleTimeMs + e.throttleTimeMs
   731	        throttledSockets += DelayedCloseSocket(socketChannel, endThrottleTimeMs)
   732	        None
   733	      case e: IOException =>
   734	        error(s"Encountered an error while configuring the connection, closing it.", e)
   735	        connectionQuotas.closeChannel(this, endPoint.listenerName, socketChannel)
   736	        None
   737	    }
   738	  }
   739	
   740	  protected def configureAcceptedSocketChannel(socketChannel: SocketChannel): Unit = {
   741	    socketChannel.configureBlocking(false)
   742	    socketChannel.socket().setTcpNoDelay(true)
   743	    socketChannel.socket().setKeepAlive(true)
   744	    if (sendBufferSize != Selectable.USE_DEFAULT_BUFFER_SIZE)
   745	      socketChannel.socket().setSendBufferSize(sendBufferSize)
   746	  }
   747	
   748	  /**
   749	   * Close sockets for any connections that have been throttled.
   750	   */
   751	  private def closeThrottledConnections(): Unit = {
   752	    val timeMs = time.milliseconds
   753	    while (throttledSockets.headOption.exists(_.endThrottleTimeMs < timeMs)) {
   754	      val closingSocket = throttledSockets.dequeue()
   755	      debug(s"Closing socket from ip ${closingSocket.socket.getRemoteAddress}")
   756	      closeSocket(closingSocket.socket)
   757	    }
   758	  }
   759	
   760	  private def assignNewConnection(socketChannel: SocketChannel, processor: Processor, mayBlock: Boolean): Boolean = {
   761	    if (processor.accept(socketChannel, mayBlock, blockedPercentMeter)) {
   762	      debug(s"Accepted connection from ${socketChannel.socket.getRemoteSocketAddress} on" +
   763	        s" ${socketChannel.socket.getLocalSocketAddress} and assigned it to processor ${processor.id}," +
   764	        s" sendBufferSize [actual|requested]: [${socketChannel.socket.getSendBufferSize}|$sendBufferSize]" +
   765	        s" recvBufferSize [actual|requested]: [${socketChannel.socket.getReceiveBufferSize}|$recvBufferSize]")
   766	      true
   767	    } else
   768	      false
   769	  }
   770	
   771	  /**
   772	   * Wakeup the thread for selection.
   773	   */
   774	  def wakeup(): Unit = nioSelector.wakeup()
   775	
   776	  def addProcessors(toCreate: Int): Unit = synchronized {
   777	    val listenerName = endPoint.listenerName
   778	    val securityProtocol = endPoint.securityProtocol
   779	    val listenerProcessors = new ArrayBuffer[Processor]()
   780	
   781	    for (_ <- 0 until toCreate) {
   782	      val processor = newProcessor(socketServer.nextProcessorId(), listenerName, securityProtocol, socketServer.connectionDisconnectListeners)
   783	      listenerProcessors += processor
   784	      requestChannel.addProcessor(processor)
   785	
   786	      if (started.get) {
   787	        processor.start()
   788	      }
   789	    }
   790	    processors ++= listenerProcessors
   791	  }
   792	
   793	  def newProcessor(id: Int,
   794	                   listenerName: ListenerName,
   795	                   securityProtocol: SecurityProtocol,
   796	                   connectionDisconnectListeners: Seq[ConnectionDisconnectListener]): Processor = {
   797	    val name = s"${threadPrefix()}-kafka-network-thread-$nodeId-${endPoint.listenerName}-${endPoint.securityProtocol}-$id"
   798	    new Processor(id,
   799	                  time,
   800	                  config.socketRequestMaxBytes,
   801	                  requestChannel,
   802	                  connectionQuotas,
   803	                  config.connectionsMaxIdleMs,
   804	                  config.failedAuthenticationDelayMs,
   805	                  listenerName,
   806	                  securityProtocol,
   807	                  endPoint,
   808	                  config,
   809	                  metrics,
   810	                  credentialProvider,
   811	                  memoryPool,
   812	                  logContext,
   813	                  Processor.ConnectionQueueSize,
   814	                  isPrivilegedListener,
   815	                  apiVersionManager,
   816	                  name,
   817	                  connectionDisconnectListeners,
   818	                  acceptorBlockedPercentMeter)
   819	  }

   995	  /**
   996	   * Build the configs map handed to IoUringPlaintextAuthenticator's principal builder,
   997	   * mirroring ChannelBuilders.channelBuilderConfigs (package-private under clients/).
   998	   *
   999	   * The NIO PlaintextChannelBuilder receives a map that merges
  1000	   * config.valuesWithPrefixOverride(listenerPrefix) (the parsed view, with per-listener
  1001	   * overrides applied) with config.originals() (the raw user-provided keys, including any
  1002	   * keys NOT in the schema such as principal.builder.* custom keys). A Configurable
  1003	   * principal builder reads this combined map in configure().
  1004	   *
  1005	   * If the io_uring path only forwarded valuesWithPrefixOverride, a custom builder whose
  1006	   * configure() reads non-schema keys would see them on a NIO listener but not on an
  1007	   * io_uring listener — a silent transport-level divergence. Replicate the merge here so
  1008	   * the two transports agree on what the user said.
  1009	   */
  1010	  private def ioUringChannelBuilderConfigs(): java.util.Map[String, _] = {
  1011	    val parsedConfigs = new java.util.HashMap[String, AnyRef]()
  1012	    parsedConfigs.putAll(config.valuesWithPrefixOverride(listenerName.configPrefix))
  1013	    val listenerPrefix = listenerName.configPrefix
  1014	    config.originals.entrySet.forEach { e =>
  1015	      val key = e.getKey
  1016	      // Skip: already in parsed map.
  1017	      if (parsedConfigs.containsKey(key)) {
  1018	        // no-op
  1019	      // Skip: listener-prefixed duplicate where the unprefixed key is already parsed.
  1020	      } else if (key.startsWith(listenerPrefix) &&
  1021	                 parsedConfigs.containsKey(key.substring(listenerPrefix.length))) {
  1022	        // no-op
  1023	      } else {
  1024	        // Mirrors ChannelBuilders.channelBuilderConfigs' third filter: drop keys whose
  1025	        // first-dot-stripped suffix is already in parsed (e.g. mechanism-prefixed keys
  1026	        // like "PLAIN.some.prop" when "some.prop" exists).
  1027	        val dotIdx = key.indexOf('.')
  1028	        if (dotIdx >= 0 && parsedConfigs.containsKey(key.substring(dotIdx + 1))) {
  1029	          // no-op
  1030	        } else {
  1031	          parsedConfigs.put(key, e.getValue)
  1032	        }
  1033	      }
  1034	    }
  1035	    parsedConfigs
  1036	  }

  1078	          applyConnectionQuotasForNewlyAcceptedChannels()
  1079	          processCompletedReceives()
  1080	          processCompletedSends()
  1081	          processDisconnected()
  1082	          closeExcessConnections()
  1083	        } catch {
  1084	          // We catch all the throwables here to prevent the processor thread from exiting. We do this because
  1085	          // letting a processor exit might cause a bigger impact on the broker. This behavior might need to be
  1086	          // reviewed if we see an exception that needs the entire broker to stop. Usually the exceptions thrown would
  1087	          // be either associated with a specific socket channel or a bad request. These exceptions are caught and
  1088	          // processed by the individual methods above which close the failing channel and continue processing other
  1089	          // channels. So this catch block should only ever see ControlThrowables.
  1090	          case e: Throwable => processException("Processor got uncaught exception.", e)
  1091	        }
  1092	      }
  1093	    } finally {
  1094	      debug(s"Closing selector - processor $id")
  1095	      CoreUtils.swallow(closeAll(), this, Level.ERROR)
  1096	    }
  1097	  }
  1098	
  1099	  private[network] def processException(errorMessage: String, throwable: Throwable): Unit = {
  1100	    throwable match {
  1101	      case e: ControlThrowable => throw e
  1102	      case e => error(errorMessage, e)
  1103	    }
  1104	  }
  1105	
  1106	  private def processChannelException(channelId: String, errorMessage: String, throwable: Throwable): Unit = {
  1107	    if (openOrClosingChannel(channelId).isDefined) {
  1108	      error(s"Closing socket for $channelId because of error", throwable)
  1109	      close(channelId)
  1110	    }
  1111	    processException(errorMessage, throwable)
  1112	  }
  1113	
  1114	  private def processNewResponses(): Unit = {
  1115	    var currentResponse: RequestChannel.Response = null
  1116	    while ({currentResponse = dequeueResponse(); currentResponse != null}) {
  1117	      val channelId = currentResponse.request.context.connectionId
  1118	      try {
  1119	        currentResponse match {
  1120	          case response: NoOpResponse =>
  1121	            // There is no response to send to the client, we need to read more pipelined requests
  1122	            // that are sitting in the server's socket buffer
  1123	            updateRequestMetrics(response)
  1124	            trace(s"Socket server received empty response to send, registering for read: $response")
  1125	            // Try unmuting the channel. If there was no quota violation and the channel has not been throttled,
  1126	            // it will be unmuted immediately. If the channel has been throttled, it will be unmuted only if the
  1127	            // throttling delay has already passed by now.
  1128	            handleChannelMuteEvent(channelId, ChannelMuteEvent.RESPONSE_SENT)
  1129	            tryUnmuteChannel(channelId)
  1130	
  1131	          case response: SendResponse =>
  1132	            sendResponse(response, response.responseSend)
  1133	          case response: CloseConnectionResponse =>
  1134	            updateRequestMetrics(response)
  1135	            trace("Closing socket connection actively according to the response code.")
  1136	            close(channelId)
  1137	          case _: StartThrottlingResponse =>
  1138	            handleChannelMuteEvent(channelId, ChannelMuteEvent.THROTTLE_STARTED)
  1139	          case _: EndThrottlingResponse =>
  1140	            // Try unmuting the channel. The channel will be unmuted only if the response has already been sent out to
  1141	            // the client.
  1142	            handleChannelMuteEvent(channelId, ChannelMuteEvent.THROTTLE_ENDED)
  1143	            tryUnmuteChannel(channelId)
  1144	          case _ =>
  1145	            throw new IllegalArgumentException(s"Unknown response type: ${currentResponse.getClass}")
  1146	        }
  1147	      } catch {
  1148	        case e: Throwable =>
  1149	          processChannelException(channelId, s"Exception while processing response for $channelId", e)
  1150	      }
  1151	    }
  1152	  }
  1153	
  1154	  // `protected` for test usage
  1155	  protected[network] def sendResponse(response: RequestChannel.Response, responseSend: Send): Unit = {
  1156	    val connectionId = response.request.context.connectionId
  1157	    trace(s"Socket server received response to send to $connectionId, registering for write and sending data: $response")
  1158	    // `channel` can be None if the connection was closed remotely or if selector closed it for being idle for too long
  1159	    if (channel(connectionId).isEmpty) {
  1160	      warn(s"Attempting to send response via channel for which there is no open connection, connection id $connectionId")
  1161	      response.request.updateRequestMetrics(0L, response)
  1162	    }
  1163	    // Invoke send for closingChannel as well so that the send is failed and the channel closed properly and
  1164	    // removed from the Selector after discarding any pending staged receives.
  1165	    // `openOrClosingChannel` can be None if the selector closed the connection because it was idle for too long
  1166	    if (openOrClosingChannel(connectionId).isDefined) {
  1167	      selector.send(new NetworkSend(connectionId, responseSend))
  1168	      inflightResponses += (connectionId -> response)
  1169	    }
  1170	  }
  1171	
  1172	  private def poll(): Unit = {
  1173	    val pollTimeout = if (newConnections.isEmpty) 300 else 0
  1174	    try selector.poll(pollTimeout)
  1175	    catch {
  1176	      case e @ (_: IllegalStateException | _: IOException) =>
  1177	        // The exception is not re-thrown and any completed sends/receives/connections/disconnections
  1178	        // from this poll will be processed.
  1179	        error(s"Processor $id poll failed", e)
  1180	    }
  1181	  }
  1182	
  1183	  private def processCompletedReceives(): Unit = {
  1184	    selector.completedReceives.forEach { receive =>
  1185	      try {
  1186	        openOrClosingChannel(receive.source) match {
  1187	          case Some(channel) =>
  1188	            val header = parseRequestHeader(apiVersionManager, receive.payload)
  1189	            if (header.apiKey == ApiKeys.SASL_HANDSHAKE && channel.maybeBeginServerReauthentication(receive,
  1190	              () => time.nanoseconds()))
  1191	              trace(s"Begin re-authentication: $channel")
  1192	            else {
  1193	              val nowNanos = time.nanoseconds()
  1194	              if (channel.serverAuthenticationSessionExpired(nowNanos)) {
  1195	                // be sure to decrease connection count and drop any in-flight responses
  1196	                debug(s"Disconnecting expired channel: $channel : $header")
  1197	                close(channel.id)
  1198	                expiredConnectionsKilledCount.record(null, 1, 0)
  1199	              } else {
  1200	                val connectionId = receive.source
  1201	                val context = new RequestContext(header, connectionId, channel.socketAddress, Optional.of(channel.socketPort()),
  1202	                  channel.principal, listenerName, securityProtocol, channel.channelMetadataRegistry.clientInformation,
  1203	                  isPrivilegedListener, channel.principalSerde)
  1204	
  1205	                val req = new RequestChannel.Request(processor = id, context = context,
  1206	                  startTimeNanos = nowNanos, memoryPool, receive.payload, requestChannel.metrics, None)
  1207	
  1208	                // KIP-511: ApiVersionsRequest is intercepted here to catch the client software name
  1209	                // and version. It is done here to avoid wiring things up to the api layer.
  1210	                if (header.apiKey == ApiKeys.API_VERSIONS) {
  1211	                  val apiVersionsRequest = req.body[ApiVersionsRequest]
  1212	                  if (apiVersionsRequest.isValid) {
  1213	                    channel.channelMetadataRegistry.registerClientInformation(new ClientInformation(
  1214	                      apiVersionsRequest.data.clientSoftwareName,
  1215	                      apiVersionsRequest.data.clientSoftwareVersion))
  1216	                  }
  1217	                }
  1218	                requestChannel.sendRequest(req)
  1219	                selector.mute(connectionId)
  1220	                handleChannelMuteEvent(connectionId, ChannelMuteEvent.REQUEST_RECEIVED)
  1221	              }
  1222	            }
  1223	          case None =>
  1224	            // This should never happen since completed receives are processed immediately after `poll()`
  1225	            throw new IllegalStateException(s"Channel ${receive.source} removed from selector before processing completed receive")
  1226	        }
  1227	      } catch {
  1228	        // note that even though we got an exception, we can assume that receive.source is valid.
  1229	        // Issues with constructing a valid receive object were handled earlier
  1230	        case e: Throwable =>
  1231	          processChannelException(receive.source, s"Exception while processing request from ${receive.source}", e)
  1232	      }
  1233	    }
  1234	    selector.clearCompletedReceives()
  1235	  }
  1236	
  1237	  private def processCompletedSends(): Unit = {
  1238	    selector.completedSends.forEach { send =>
  1239	      try {
  1240	        val response = inflightResponses.remove(send.destinationId).getOrElse {
  1241	          throw new IllegalStateException(s"Send for ${send.destinationId} completed, but not in `inflightResponses`")
  1242	        }
  1243	
  1244	        // Invoke send completion callback, and then update request metrics since there might be some
  1245	        // request metrics got updated during callback
  1246	        response.onComplete.foreach(onComplete => onComplete(send))
  1247	        updateRequestMetrics(response)
  1248	
  1249	        // Try unmuting the channel. If there was no quota violation and the channel has not been throttled,
  1250	        // it will be unmuted immediately. If the channel has been throttled, it will unmuted only if the throttling
  1251	        // delay has already passed by now.
  1252	        handleChannelMuteEvent(send.destinationId, ChannelMuteEvent.RESPONSE_SENT)
  1253	        tryUnmuteChannel(send.destinationId)
  1254	      } catch {
  1255	        case e: Throwable => processChannelException(send.destinationId,
  1256	          s"Exception while processing completed send to ${send.destinationId}", e)
  1257	      }
  1258	    }
  1259	    selector.clearCompletedSends()
  1260	  }
  1261	
  1262	  private def updateRequestMetrics(response: RequestChannel.Response): Unit = {
  1263	    val request = response.request
  1264	    val networkThreadTimeNanos = openOrClosingChannel(request.context.connectionId).fold(0L)(_.getAndResetNetworkThreadTimeNanos())
  1265	    request.updateRequestMetrics(networkThreadTimeNanos, response)
  1266	  }
  1267	
  1268	  /**
  1269	   * Apply ConnectionQuotas.inc for channels that just surfaced from selector.connected().
  1270	   * io_uring listeners accept inside Netty's event loop and never went through
  1271	   * Acceptor.accept (which is where NIO inc'd the quota), so the gate has to land here
  1272	   * instead.
  1273	   *
  1274	   * Refusal paths:
  1275	   *  - tryInc returns false (broker max-connections hit, or listener connection-rate
  1276	   *    throttle): no inc happened, so we just close the selector channel.
  1277	   *  - TooManyConnectionsException: tryInc increments the per-IP / listener / total
  1278	   *    counters BEFORE checking the per-IP max (see ConnectionQuotas.tryInc), so by
  1279	   *    the time the exception fires the inc has already landed. selector.close on the
  1280	   *    io_uring path is silent (it does NOT surface in selector.disconnected, so

 succeeded in 0ms:
  1268	  /**
  1269	   * Apply ConnectionQuotas.inc for channels that just surfaced from selector.connected().
  1270	   * io_uring listeners accept inside Netty's event loop and never went through
  1271	   * Acceptor.accept (which is where NIO inc'd the quota), so the gate has to land here
  1272	   * instead.
  1273	   *
  1274	   * Refusal paths:
  1275	   *  - tryInc returns false (broker max-connections hit, or listener connection-rate
  1276	   *    throttle): no inc happened, so we just close the selector channel.
  1277	   *  - TooManyConnectionsException: tryInc increments the per-IP / listener / total
  1278	   *    counters BEFORE checking the per-IP max (see ConnectionQuotas.tryInc), so by
  1279	   *    the time the exception fires the inc has already landed. selector.close on the
  1280	   *    io_uring path is silent (it does NOT surface in selector.disconnected, so
  1281	   *    processDisconnected will not run the normal dec). Roll the inc back here.
  1282	   *  - ConnectionThrottledException: thrown by recordIpConnectionMaybeThrottle, which
  1283	   *    runs before the inc, so no rollback is needed.
  1284	   *
  1285	   * For NIO listeners selector.connected() is always empty (broker-side Processors don't
  1286	   * initiate outgoing connections), so this method is a hot-path no-op there.
  1287	   */
  1288	  private def applyConnectionQuotasForNewlyAcceptedChannels(): Unit = {
  1289	    val newlyConnected = selector.connected()
  1290	    if (newlyConnected.isEmpty) return
  1291	    newlyConnected.forEach { connectionId =>
  1292	      val channel = selector.channel(connectionId)
  1293	      if (channel != null) {
  1294	        val address = channel.socketAddress
  1295	        if (address != null) {
  1296	          try {
  1297	            // tryInc — never blocks. On NIO the dedicated Acceptor thread can wait for a
  1298	            // slot via inc(); here the caller is the Processor's poll loop, so a wait
  1299	            // would stall every other channel on this Processor.
  1300	            if (!connectionQuotas.tryInc(listenerName, address)) {
  1301	              info(s"Closing io_uring connection $connectionId from $address: broker- or " +
  1302	                s"listener-level connection slot unavailable or rate-limited.")
  1303	              selector.close(connectionId)
  1304	            }
  1305	          } catch {
  1306	            case e: TooManyConnectionsException =>
  1307	              info(s"Closing io_uring connection $connectionId from ${e.ip}: " +
  1308	                s"already has the configured maximum of ${e.count} connections.")
  1309	              // tryInc incremented counts before throwing; selector.close is silent on
  1310	              // the io_uring path, so processDisconnected will not run dec. Roll back
  1311	              // the inc explicitly, otherwise the per-IP / listener / total counters
  1312	              // leak permanently and the next refusal compares against a stale count.
  1313	              connectionQuotas.dec(listenerName, address)
  1314	              selector.close(connectionId)
  1315	            case e: ConnectionThrottledException =>
  1316	              debug(s"Closing throttled io_uring connection $connectionId from $address " +
  1317	                s"(throttle ${e.throttleTimeMs}ms)")
  1318	              selector.close(connectionId)
  1319	          }
  1320	        }
  1321	      }
  1322	    }
  1323	  }
  1324	
  1325	  private def processDisconnected(): Unit = {
  1326	    selector.disconnected.keySet.forEach { connectionId =>
  1327	      try {
  1328	        val remoteHost = ServerConnectionId.fromString(connectionId).orElseThrow { () =>
  1329	          throw new IllegalStateException(s"connectionId has unexpected format: $connectionId")
  1330	        }.remoteHost
  1331	        inflightResponses.remove(connectionId).foreach(updateRequestMetrics)
  1332	        // the channel has been closed by the selector but the quotas still need to be updated
  1333	        connectionQuotas.dec(listenerName, InetAddress.getByName(remoteHost))
  1334	        // Call listeners to notify for closed connection.
  1335	        connectionDisconnectListeners.foreach(listener => CoreUtils.swallow(() -> listener.onDisconnect(connectionId), this, Level.ERROR))
  1336	      } catch {
  1337	        case e: Throwable => processException(s"Exception while processing disconnection of $connectionId", e)
  1338	      }
  1339	    }
  1340	  }
  1341	
  1342	  private def closeExcessConnections(): Unit = {
  1343	    if (connectionQuotas.maxConnectionsExceeded(listenerName)) {
  1344	      val channel = selector.lowestPriorityChannel()
  1345	      if (channel != null)
  1346	        close(channel.id)
  1347	    }
  1348	  }
  1349	
  1350	  /**
  1351	   * Close the connection identified by `connectionId` and decrement the connection count.
  1352	   * The channel will be immediately removed from the selector's `channels` or `closingChannels`
  1353	   * and no further disconnect notifications will be sent for this channel by the selector.
  1354	   * If responses are pending for the channel, they are dropped and metrics is updated.
  1355	   * If the channel has already been removed from selector, no action is taken.
  1356	   */
  1357	  private def close(connectionId: String): Unit = {
  1358	    openOrClosingChannel(connectionId).foreach { channel =>
  1359	      debug(s"Closing selector connection $connectionId")
  1360	      val address = channel.socketAddress
  1361	      if (address != null)
  1362	        connectionQuotas.dec(listenerName, address)
  1363	      selector.close(connectionId)
  1364	      // Call listeners to notify for closed connection.
  1365	      connectionDisconnectListeners.foreach(listener => CoreUtils.swallow(() -> listener.onDisconnect(connectionId), this, Level.ERROR))
  1366	
  1367	      inflightResponses.remove(connectionId).foreach(response => updateRequestMetrics(response))
  1368	    }
  1369	  }
  1370	
  1371	  /**
  1372	   * Queue up a new connection for reading
  1373	   */
  1374	  def accept(socketChannel: SocketChannel,
  1375	             mayBlock: Boolean,
  1376	             acceptorIdlePercentMeter: com.yammer.metrics.core.Meter): Boolean = {
  1377	    val accepted = {
  1378	      if (newConnections.offer(socketChannel))
  1379	        true
  1380	      else if (mayBlock) {
  1381	        val startNs = time.nanoseconds
  1382	        newConnections.put(socketChannel)
  1383	        acceptorIdlePercentMeter.mark(time.nanoseconds() - startNs)
  1384	        true
  1385	      } else
  1386	        false
  1387	    }
  1388	    if (accepted)
  1389	      wakeup()
  1390	    accepted
  1391	  }
  1392	
  1393	  /**
  1394	   * Register any new connections that have been queued up. The number of connections processed
  1395	   * in each iteration is limited to ensure that traffic and connection close notifications of
  1396	   * existing channels are handled promptly.
  1397	   */
  1398	  private def configureNewConnections(): Unit = {
  1399	    var connectionsProcessed = 0
  1400	    while (connectionsProcessed < connectionQueueSize && !newConnections.isEmpty) {
  1401	      val channel = newConnections.poll()
  1402	      try {
  1403	        debug(s"Processor $id listening to new connection from ${channel.socket.getRemoteSocketAddress}")
  1404	        selector.register(connectionId(channel.socket), channel)
  1405	        connectionsProcessed += 1
  1406	      } catch {
  1407	        // We explicitly catch all exceptions and close the socket to avoid a socket leak.
  1408	        case e: Throwable =>
  1409	          val remoteAddress = channel.socket.getRemoteSocketAddress
  1410	          // need to close the channel here to avoid a socket leak.
  1411	          connectionQuotas.closeChannel(this, listenerName, channel)
  1412	          processException(s"Processor $id closed connection from $remoteAddress", e)
  1413	      }
  1414	    }
  1415	  }
  1416	
  1417	  /**
  1418	   * Close the selector and all open connections. For io_uring listeners we tear down the
  1419	   * IoUringServerListener first so no new accepts surface mid-shutdown, then drain pending
  1420	   * channels through the usual selector.close(id) path, then close the selector itself.
  1421	   */
  1422	  private def closeAll(): Unit = {
  1423	    ioBundle.listener.foreach { l =>
  1424	      try l.close()
  1425	      catch { case t: Throwable => error("Error closing IoUringServerListener", t) }
  1426	    }
  1427	    while (!newConnections.isEmpty) {
  1428	      newConnections.poll().close()
  1429	    }
  1430	    selector.channels.forEach { channel =>
  1431	      close(channel.id)
  1432	    }
  1433	    selector.close()
  1434	    metricsGroup.removeMetric(IdlePercentMetricName, Map(NetworkProcessorMetricTag -> id.toString).asJava)
  1435	  }
  1436	
  1437	  // 'protected` to allow override for testing
  1438	  protected[network] def connectionId(socket: Socket): String = {
  1439	    val connId = ServerConnectionId.generateConnectionId(socket, id, nextConnectionIndex)
  1440	    nextConnectionIndex = if (nextConnectionIndex == Int.MaxValue) 0 else nextConnectionIndex + 1
  1441	    connId
  1442	  }

  1445	    responseQueue.put(response)

  1445	    responseQueue.put(response)
  1446	    wakeup()
  1447	  }

  1449	  private def dequeueResponse(): RequestChannel.Response = {
  1450	    val response = responseQueue.poll()
  1451	    if (response != null)
  1452	      response.request.responseDequeueTimeNanos = Time.SYSTEM.nanoseconds
  1453	    response
  1454	  }
  1455	
  1456	  private[network] def responseQueueSize = responseQueue.size
  1457	
  1458	  // Only for testing
  1459	  private[network] def inflightResponseCount: Int = inflightResponses.size

  1461	  // Visible for testing
  1462	  // Only methods that are safe to call on a disconnected channel should be invoked on 'openOrClosingChannel'.
  1463	  private[network] def openOrClosingChannel(connectionId: String): Option[KafkaChannel] =
  1464	    Option(selector.channel(connectionId)).orElse(Option(selector.closingChannel(connectionId)))
  1465	
  1466	  // Indicate the specified channel that the specified channel mute-related event has happened so that it can change its
  1467	  // mute state.
  1468	  private def handleChannelMuteEvent(connectionId: String, event: ChannelMuteEvent): Unit = {
  1469	    openOrClosingChannel(connectionId).foreach(c => c.handleChannelMuteEvent(event))
  1470	  }
  1471	
  1472	  private def tryUnmuteChannel(connectionId: String): Unit = {
  1473	    openOrClosingChannel(connectionId).foreach(c => selector.unmute(c.id))
  1474	  }

  1476	  /* For test usage */
  1477	  private[network] def channel(connectionId: String): Option[KafkaChannel] =
  1478	    Option(selector.channel(connectionId))

  1480	  def start(): Unit = {
  1481	    if (!started.getAndSet(true)) {
  1482	      thread.start()
  1483	    }
  1484	  }
  1485	
  1486	  /**
  1487	   * Wakeup the thread for selection.
  1488	   */
  1489	  def wakeup(): Unit = selector.wakeup()
  1490	
  1491	  def beginShutdown(): Unit = {
  1492	    if (shouldRun.getAndSet(false)) {
  1493	      wakeup()
  1494	    }
  1495	  }
  1496	
  1497	  def close(): Unit = {
  1498	    try {
  1499	      beginShutdown()
  1500	      thread.join()
  1501	      if (!started.get) {
  1502	        CoreUtils.swallow(closeAll(), this, Level.ERROR)
  1503	      }
  1504	    } finally {
  1505	      metricsGroup.removeMetric("IdlePercent", Map("networkProcessor" -> id.toString).asJava)
  1506	      metrics.removeMetric(expiredConnectionsKilledCountMetricName)
  1507	    }
  1508	  }
  1509	}
  1510	
  1511	class ConnectionQuotas(config: KafkaConfig, time: Time, metrics: Metrics) extends Logging with AutoCloseable {
  1512	
  1513	  @volatile private var defaultMaxConnectionsPerIp: Int = config.maxConnectionsPerIp
  1514	  @volatile private var maxConnectionsPerIpOverrides = config.maxConnectionsPerIpOverrides.map { case (host, count) => (InetAddress.getByName(host), count) }
  1515	  @volatile private var brokerMaxConnections = config.maxConnections
  1516	  private val interBrokerListenerName = config.interBrokerListenerName
  1517	  private val counts = mutable.Map[InetAddress, Int]()
  1518	
  1519	  // Listener counts and configs are synchronized on `counts`
  1520	  private val listenerCounts = mutable.Map[ListenerName, Int]()
  1521	  private[network] val maxConnectionsPerListener = mutable.Map[ListenerName, ListenerConnectionQuota]()
  1522	  @volatile private var totalCount = 0
  1523	  // updates to defaultConnectionRatePerIp or connectionRatePerIp must be synchronized on `counts`
  1524	  @volatile private var defaultConnectionRatePerIp = QuotaConfig.IP_CONNECTION_RATE_DEFAULT.intValue()
  1525	  private val connectionRatePerIp = new ConcurrentHashMap[InetAddress, Int]()
  1526	  // sensor that tracks broker-wide connection creation rate and limit (quota)
  1527	  private val brokerConnectionRateSensor = getOrCreateConnectionRateQuotaSensor(config.maxConnectionCreationRate, ConnectionQuotaEntity.brokerQuotaEntity())
  1528	  private val maxThrottleTimeMs = TimeUnit.SECONDS.toMillis(config.quotaConfig.quotaWindowSizeSeconds.toLong)
  1529	
  1530	  def inc(listenerName: ListenerName, address: InetAddress, acceptorBlockedPercentMeter: com.yammer.metrics.core.Meter): Unit = {
  1531	    counts.synchronized {
  1532	      waitForConnectionSlot(listenerName, acceptorBlockedPercentMeter)
  1533	
  1534	      recordIpConnectionMaybeThrottle(listenerName, address)
  1535	      val count = counts.getOrElseUpdate(address, 0)
  1536	      counts.put(address, count + 1)
  1537	      totalCount += 1
  1538	      if (listenerCounts.contains(listenerName)) {
  1539	        listenerCounts.put(listenerName, listenerCounts(listenerName) + 1)
  1540	      }
  1541	      val max = maxConnectionsPerIpOverrides.getOrElse(address, defaultMaxConnectionsPerIp)
  1542	      if (count >= max)
  1543	        throw new TooManyConnectionsException(address, max)
  1544	    }
  1545	  }
  1546	
  1547	  /**
  1548	   * Non-blocking variant of [[inc]] used by the io_uring Processor accept path.
  1549	   *
  1550	   * On the NIO path [[inc]] blocks the dedicated Acceptor thread when the broker is at
  1551	   * max.connections or when the listener connection-rate quota requires a throttle.
  1552	   * On the io_uring path the caller is the Processor's poll loop — blocking it would
  1553	   * stall every other channel on that Processor, including in-flight requests. This
  1554	   * method instead refuses (returns false) when the slot is unavailable or a throttle
  1555	   * applies, after un-recording the rate-sensor sample so accounting stays correct.
  1556	   *
  1557	   * Like [[inc]], may throw [[TooManyConnectionsException]] (per-IP max) or
  1558	   * [[ConnectionThrottledException]] (per-IP rate); callers MUST close the channel on
  1559	   * either return-false or exception.
  1560	   */
  1561	  def tryInc(listenerName: ListenerName, address: InetAddress): Boolean = {
  1562	    counts.synchronized {
  1563	      val timeMs = time.milliseconds
  1564	      val throttleTimeMs = math.max(recordConnectionAndGetThrottleTimeMs(listenerName, timeMs), 0)
  1565	      if (throttleTimeMs > 0 || !connectionSlotAvailable(listenerName)) {
  1566	        // Un-record the speculative +1 from recordConnectionAndGetThrottleTimeMs so the
  1567	        // rate sensor doesn't drift when we refuse the connection. Mirrors the un-record
  1568	        // path used by recordIpConnectionMaybeThrottle for IP throttle refusal.
  1569	        if (!protectedListener(listenerName)) {
  1570	          brokerConnectionRateSensor.record(-1.0, timeMs, false)
  1571	        }
  1572	        maxConnectionsPerListener
  1573	          .get(listenerName)
  1574	          .foreach(_.connectionRateSensor.record(-1.0, timeMs, false))
  1575	        return false
  1576	      }
  1577	
  1578	      recordIpConnectionMaybeThrottle(listenerName, address)
  1579	      val count = counts.getOrElseUpdate(address, 0)
  1580	      counts.put(address, count + 1)
  1581	      totalCount += 1
  1582	      if (listenerCounts.contains(listenerName)) {
  1583	        listenerCounts.put(listenerName, listenerCounts(listenerName) + 1)
  1584	      }
  1585	      val max = maxConnectionsPerIpOverrides.getOrElse(address, defaultMaxConnectionsPerIp)
  1586	      if (count >= max)
  1587	        throw new TooManyConnectionsException(address, max)
  1588	      true
  1589	    }
  1590	  }
  1591	
  1592	  private[network] def updateMaxConnectionsPerIp(maxConnectionsPerIp: Int): Unit = {
  1593	    defaultMaxConnectionsPerIp = maxConnectionsPerIp
  1594	  }
  1595	
  1596	  private[network] def updateMaxConnectionsPerIpOverride(overrideQuotas: Map[String, Int]): Unit = {
  1597	    maxConnectionsPerIpOverrides = overrideQuotas.map { case (host, count) => (InetAddress.getByName(host), count) }
  1598	  }
  1599	
  1600	  private[network] def updateBrokerMaxConnections(maxConnections: Int): Unit = {
  1601	    counts.synchronized {
  1602	      brokerMaxConnections = maxConnections
  1603	      counts.notifyAll()
  1604	    }
  1605	  }

   153	  // Processors are now created by each Acceptor. However to preserve compatibility, we need to number the processors
   154	  // globally, so we keep the nextProcessorId counter in SocketServer
   155	  def nextProcessorId(): Int = {
   156	    nextProcessorId.getAndIncrement()
   157	  }

   159	  /**
   160	   * This method enables request processing for all endpoints managed by this SocketServer. Each
   161	   * endpoint will be brought up asynchronously as soon as its associated future is completed.
   162	   * Therefore, we do not know that any particular request processor will be running by the end of
   163	   * this function -- just that it might be running.
   164	   *
   165	   * @param authorizerFutures     Future per [[EndPoint]] used to wait before starting the
   166	   *                              processor corresponding to the [[EndPoint]]. Any endpoint
   167	   *                              that does not appear in this map will be started once all
   168	   *                              authorizerFutures are complete.
   169	   *
   170	   * @return                      A future which is completed when all of the acceptor threads have
   171	   *                              successfully started. If any of them do not start, the future will
   172	   *                              be completed with an exception.
   173	   */
   174	  def enableRequestProcessing(
   175	    authorizerFutures: Map[Endpoint, CompletableFuture[Void]]
   176	  ): CompletableFuture[Void] = this.synchronized {
   177	    if (stopped) {
   178	      throw new RuntimeException("Can't enable request processing: SocketServer is stopped.")
   179	    }
   180	
   181	    def chainAcceptorFuture(acceptor: Acceptor): Unit = {
   182	      // Because of ephemeral ports, we need to match acceptors to futures by looking at
   183	      // the listener name, rather than the endpoint object.
   184	      val authorizerFuture = authorizerFutures.find {
   185	        case (endpoint, _) => acceptor.endPoint.listenerName.value().equals(endpoint.listenerName().get())
   186	      } match {
   187	        case None => allAuthorizerFuturesComplete
   188	        case Some((_, future)) => future
   189	      }
   190	      authorizerFuture.whenComplete((_, e) => {
   191	        if (e != null) {
   192	          // If the authorizer failed to start, fail the acceptor's startedFuture.
   193	          acceptor.startedFuture.completeExceptionally(e)
   194	        } else {
   195	          // Once the authorizer has started, attempt to start the associated acceptor. The Acceptor.start()
   196	          // function will complete the acceptor started future (either successfully or not)
   197	          acceptor.start()
   198	        }
   199	      })
   200	    }
   201	
   202	    info("Enabling request processing.")
   203	    dataPlaneAcceptors.values().forEach(chainAcceptorFuture)
   204	    FutureUtils.chainFuture(CompletableFuture.allOf(authorizerFutures.values.toArray: _*),
   205	        allAuthorizerFuturesComplete)
   206	
   207	    // Construct a future that will be completed when all Acceptors have been successfully started.
   208	    // Alternately, if any of them fail to start, this future will be completed exceptionally.
   209	    val enableFuture = new CompletableFuture[Void]
   210	    FutureUtils.chainFuture(CompletableFuture.allOf(dataPlaneAcceptors.values().asScala.toArray.map(_.startedFuture): _*), enableFuture)
   211	    enableFuture
   212	  }
   213	
   214	  private def createDataPlaneAcceptorAndProcessors(endpoint: EndPoint): Unit = synchronized {
   215	    if (stopped) {
   216	      throw new RuntimeException("Can't create new data plane acceptor and processors: SocketServer is stopped.")
   217	    }
   218	    val parsedConfigs = config.valuesFromThisConfigWithPrefixOverride(endpoint.listenerName.configPrefix)
   219	    connectionQuotas.addListener(config, endpoint.listenerName)
   220	    val isPrivilegedListener = config.interBrokerListenerName == endpoint.listenerName
   221	    val dataPlaneAcceptor = createDataPlaneAcceptor(endpoint, isPrivilegedListener, dataPlaneRequestChannel)
   222	    config.addReconfigurable(dataPlaneAcceptor)
   223	    dataPlaneAcceptor.configure(parsedConfigs)
   224	    dataPlaneAcceptors.put(endpoint, dataPlaneAcceptor)
   225	    info(s"Created data-plane acceptor and processors for endpoint : ${endpoint.listenerName}")
   226	  }
   227	
   228	  private def endpoints = config.listeners.map(l => l.listenerName -> l).toMap
   229

   239	      stopped = true

   822	/**
   823	 * The pair of I/O objects a Processor owns. For NIO listeners {@code listener} is None and
   824	 * the Acceptor binds the server socket. For io_uring listeners {@code listener} is Some and
   825	 * holds the per-Processor IoUringServerListener (bound on the same address via
   826	 * SO_REUSEPORT). Closing the bundle tears the listener down before the selector so accepted
   827	 * channels still flow through {@code onDisconnect} before the selector is gone.
   828	 */
   829	private[network] case class ProcessorIoBundle(
   830	  selector: BrokerSelector,
   831	  listener: Option[org.apache.kafka.network.iouring.IoUringServerListener]) {
   832	  def close(): Unit = {
   833	    listener.foreach(l => try l.close() catch { case _: Throwable => /* logged downstream */ })
   834	    selector.close()
   835	  }
   836	}

   838	private[kafka] object Processor {
   839	  private val IdlePercentMetricName = "IdlePercent"

   844	  private[network] def parseRequestHeader(apiVersionManager: ApiVersionManager, buffer: ByteBuffer): RequestHeader = {
   845	    val header = RequestHeader.parse(buffer)
   846	    if (apiVersionManager.isApiEnabled(header.apiKey, header.apiVersion)) {
   847	      header
   848	    } else if (header.isApiVersionSupported()) {
   849	      throw new InvalidRequestException(s"Received request for disabled api with key ${header.apiKey.id} (${header.apiKey().name}) and version ${header.apiVersion}")
   850	    } else {
   851	      throw new UnsupportedVersionException(s"Received request for api with key ${header.apiKey.id} (${header.apiKey().name}) and unsupported version ${header.apiVersion}")
   852	    }
   853	  }
   854	}

    26	/**
    27	 * Broker-side facade over {@link Selectable}. Captures the additional methods that the
    28	 * {@code kafka.network.Processor} relies on beyond the public {@link Selectable} interface,
    29	 * so an alternative I/O backend (Netty io_uring) can be plugged in without modifying
    30	 * {@code clients/}.
    31	 *
    32	 * <p>The contract is identical to {@code org.apache.kafka.common.network.Selector} for the
    33	 * methods listed below — see that class for invariants. Implementations are not required to
    34	 * be thread-safe; each instance is owned by a single Processor thread.
    35	 */
    36	public interface BrokerSelector extends Selectable, AutoCloseable {
    37	
    38	    /**
    39	     * Register an already-accepted server-side socket channel with the selector. The selector
    40	     * takes ownership of the channel; subsequent I/O is driven through {@link #poll(long)}.
    41	     *
    42	     * @throws IllegalStateException if a channel with this id is already registered or closing
    43	     */
    44	    void register(String id, SocketChannel socketChannel) throws IOException;
    45	
    46	    /** All channels currently registered with the selector (including closing channels in some backends). */
    47	    List<KafkaChannel> channels();
    48	
    49	    /** Look up an active channel by id, or {@code null} if none. */
    50	    KafkaChannel channel(String id);
    51	
    52	    /** Look up a channel that is in the process of being closed by id, or {@code null} if none. */
    53	    KafkaChannel closingChannel(String id);
    54	
    55	    /**
    56	     * The channel with the lowest priority (typically least-recently-used), used by the broker
    57	     * to make room when global connection quotas are exceeded.
    58	     *
    59	     * @return the lowest-priority channel, or {@code null} if no eligible channel exists
    60	     */
    61	    KafkaChannel lowestPriorityChannel();
    62	
    63	    /**
    64	     * Discard the receive-completion events produced by the most recent {@link #poll(long)}.
    65	     * Called by the broker once it has handed those receives off to request processing.
    66	     */
    67	    void clearCompletedReceives();
    68	
    69	    /**
    70	     * Discard the send-completion events produced by the most recent {@link #poll(long)}.
    71	     * Called by the broker once it has handed those sends off to response processing.
    72	     */
    73	    void clearCompletedSends();
    74	
    75	    @Override
    76	    void close();
    77	}

    17	package org.apache.kafka.network.iouring;
    18	
    19	import org.apache.kafka.common.config.ConfigException;
    20	import org.apache.kafka.network.SocketServerConfigs;

    26	/**
    27	 * Which broker-side I/O backend to use for accepting and serving client connections.
    28	 *
    29	 * <p>Selection happens once at broker startup; there is no per-request dispatch.
    30	 * {@link #AUTO} resolves to {@link #IO_URING} on a Linux host with kernel io_uring support and
    31	 * a PLAINTEXT listener, and to {@link #NIO} everywhere else.
    32	 */
    33	public enum SelectorImplementation {
    34	    /** Java NIO Selector-based path; the historical Kafka default. */
    35	    NIO("nio"),
    36	    /** Netty io_uring-backed path; Linux + PLAINTEXT only in this version. */
    37	    IO_URING("io_uring"),
    38	    /** Pick {@link #IO_URING} when supported, otherwise {@link #NIO}. */
    39	    AUTO("auto");
    40	
    41	    private final String configValue;
    42	
    43	    SelectorImplementation(String configValue) {
    44	        this.configValue = configValue;
    45	    }
    46	
    47	    public String configValue() {
    48	        return configValue;
    49	    }
    50	
    51	    public static SelectorImplementation fromConfig(String value) {
    52	        if (value == null || value.trim().isEmpty()) {
    53	            throw new ConfigException(SocketServerConfigs.SOCKET_SELECTOR_IMPLEMENTATION_CONFIG,
    54	                value, "must be one of " + allowedValues());
    55	        }
    56	        String normalized = value.trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
    57	        for (SelectorImplementation impl : values()) {
    58	            if (impl.configValue.equals(normalized)) {
    59	                return impl;
    60	            }
    61	        }
    62	        throw new ConfigException(SocketServerConfigs.SOCKET_SELECTOR_IMPLEMENTATION_CONFIG,
    63	            value, "must be one of " + allowedValues());
    64	    }
    65	
    66	    private static String allowedValues() {
    67	        return Arrays.stream(values())
    68	            .map(SelectorImplementation::configValue)
    69	            .collect(Collectors.joining(", "));
    70	    }
    71	}

     1	/*
