    17	package org.apache.kafka.network.iouring;
    18	
    19	import org.apache.kafka.common.security.auth.SecurityProtocol;
    20	import org.apache.kafka.network.SocketServerConfigs;
    21	
    22	/**
    23	 * Decision function that maps the operator-requested
    24	 * {@link SelectorImplementation}, the listener's {@link SecurityProtocol}, and a
    25	 * platform availability flag to the effective implementation a per-listener
    26	 * Processor should construct.
    27	 *
    28	 * <p>Rules (v1):
    29	 * <ul>
    30	 *   <li>Explicit {@code nio} always wins.</li>
    31	 *   <li>io_uring is gated on PLAINTEXT only; SSL / SASL_PLAINTEXT / SASL_SSL transparently
    32	 *       fall back to NIO regardless of the request.</li>
    33	 *   <li>{@code auto} resolves to io_uring iff the platform supports it; otherwise NIO.</li>
    34	 *   <li>Explicit {@code io_uring} on a PLAINTEXT listener with no platform support is a
    35	 *       hard error — the operator asked for io_uring; silently downgrading would hide
    36	 *       configuration drift.</li>
    37	 * </ul>
    38	 */
    39	public final class BrokerSelectorFactory {
    40	
    41	    private BrokerSelectorFactory() { }
    42	
    43	    /**
    44	     * @param requested  operator's parsed {@link SelectorImplementation} config value
    45	     * @param protocol   the listener's security protocol
    46	     * @param ioUringAvailable platform probe result — typically {@link IoUringSupport#isAvailable()}
    47	     * @return the effective implementation to construct
    48	     * @throws IllegalStateException when {@code io_uring} is explicitly requested for a
    49	     *         PLAINTEXT listener but the host has no io_uring support
    50	     */
    51	    public static SelectorImplementation resolve(SelectorImplementation requested,
    52	                                                 SecurityProtocol protocol,
    53	                                                 boolean ioUringAvailable) {
    54	        if (requested == SelectorImplementation.NIO) {
    55	            return SelectorImplementation.NIO;
    56	        }
    57	        // v1 PLAINTEXT-only constraint: any non-PLAINTEXT listener transparently uses NIO.
    58	        if (protocol != SecurityProtocol.PLAINTEXT) {
    59	            return SelectorImplementation.NIO;
    60	        }
    61	        if (requested == SelectorImplementation.AUTO) {
    62	            return ioUringAvailable ? SelectorImplementation.IO_URING : SelectorImplementation.NIO;
    63	        }
    64	        // Explicit IO_URING on a PLAINTEXT listener.
    65	        if (!ioUringAvailable) {
    66	            String reason = IoUringSupport.unavailabilityReason();
    67	            throw new IllegalStateException(
    68	                SocketServerConfigs.SOCKET_SELECTOR_IMPLEMENTATION_CONFIG
    69	                    + "=" + SelectorImplementation.IO_URING.configValue()
    70	                    + " was requested but io_uring is unavailable on this host: "
    71	                    + (reason != null ? reason : "unknown reason"));
    72	        }
    73	        return SelectorImplementation.IO_URING;
    74	    }
    75	}

exec

    17	package org.apache.kafka.network.iouring;
    18	
    19	import org.slf4j.Logger;
    20	import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
    21	
    22	import java.lang.reflect.Method;
    23	import java.util.Locale;
    24	
    25	/**
    26	 * Runtime probe for whether the Netty io_uring transport is usable on this JVM/host.
    27	 *
    28	 * <p>The probe is reflective so this class itself loads on any platform — including
    29	 * macOS / Windows / Linux JVMs that don't ship the io_uring native library — without
    30	 * triggering a NoClassDefFoundError.
    31	 *
    32	 * <p>The result is cached; the underlying conditions (OS, kernel, native lib) don't
    33	 * change during a JVM run.
    34	 */
    35	public final class IoUringSupport {
    36	
    37	    private static final Logger LOG = LoggerFactory.getLogger(IoUringSupport.class);
    38	    private static final String IO_URING_PROBE_CLASS = "io.netty.channel.uring.IoUring";
    39	
    40	    private static final boolean LINUX =
    41	        System.getProperty("os.name", "").toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).contains("linux");
    42	
    43	    private static final Probe PROBE = computeProbe();
    44	
    45	    private IoUringSupport() { }
    46	
    47	    /** @return true iff this JVM is running on Linux. */
    48	    public static boolean isLinux() {
    49	        return LINUX;
    50	    }
    51	
    52	    /**
    53	     * @return true iff the Netty io_uring transport reports itself usable on this host
    54	     *         (Linux kernel with io_uring support and the native lib loadable).
    55	     */
    56	    public static boolean isAvailable() {
    57	        return PROBE.available;
    58	    }
    59	
    60	    /**
    61	     * @return a human-readable reason why io_uring is unavailable, or {@code null} when
    62	     *         {@link #isAvailable()} is {@code true}. The reason is suitable for inclusion
    63	     *         in startup logs and error messages.
    64	     */
    65	    public static String unavailabilityReason() {
    66	        return PROBE.reason;
    67	    }
    68	
    69	    private static Probe computeProbe() {
    70	        if (!LINUX) {
    71	            return new Probe(false, "io_uring requires Linux (this JVM reports os.name="
    72	                + System.getProperty("os.name") + ")");
    73	        }
    74	        try {
    75	            Class<?> ioUring = Class.forName(IO_URING_PROBE_CLASS, true, IoUringSupport.class.getClassLoader());
    76	            Method isAvailable = ioUring.getMethod("isAvailable");
    77	            boolean available = (Boolean) isAvailable.invoke(null);
    78	            if (available) {
    79	                return new Probe(true, null);
    80	            }
    81	            String cause = describeUnavailabilityCause(ioUring);
    82	            return new Probe(false, "Netty io_uring transport reports unavailable: " + cause);
    83	        } catch (ClassNotFoundException e) {
    84	            return new Probe(false, "Netty io_uring transport classes not on the classpath ("
    85	                + IO_URING_PROBE_CLASS + ")");
    86	        } catch (ReflectiveOperationException e) {
    87	            LOG.debug("Failed to probe Netty io_uring availability via reflection", e);
    88	            return new Probe(false, "Reflective probe of " + IO_URING_PROBE_CLASS
    89	                + " failed: " + e.getClass().getSimpleName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
    90	        }
    91	    }
    92	
    93	    private static String describeUnavailabilityCause(Class<?> ioUring) {
    94	        try {
    95	            Method unavailabilityCause = ioUring.getMethod("unavailabilityCause");
    96	            Object cause = unavailabilityCause.invoke(null);
    97	            if (cause == null) return "no cause reported";
    98	            return cause.getClass().getSimpleName() + ": " + ((Throwable) cause).getMessage();
    99	        } catch (ReflectiveOperationException e) {
   100	            return "unavailabilityCause() unreadable (" + e.getClass().getSimpleName() + ")";
   101	        }
   102	    }
   103	
   104	    private static final class Probe {
   105	        final boolean available;
   106	        final String reason;
   107	        Probe(boolean available, String reason) {
   108	            this.available = available;
   109	            this.reason = reason;
   110	        }
   111	    }
   112	}

The wiring chooses io_uring only through `KafkaConfig.usesIoUring`, and the Processor constructs an io_uring selector/listener pair only on that resolved path. I’m comparing the selector loop and teardown semantics against the actual NIO selector next, because most production surprises show up in those edge transitions.

   296	    /**
   297	     * Register the nioSelector with an existing channel
   298	     * Use this on server-side, when a connection is accepted by a different thread but processed by the Selector
   299	     * <p>
   300	     * If a connection already exists with the same connection id in `channels` or `closingChannels`,
   301	     * an exception is thrown. Connection ids must be chosen to avoid conflict when remote ports are reused.
   302	     * Kafka brokers add an incrementing index to the connection id to avoid reuse in the timing window
   303	     * where an existing connection may not yet have been closed by the broker when a new connection with
   304	     * the same remote host:port is processed.
   305	     * </p><p>
   306	     * If a `KafkaChannel` cannot be created for this connection, the `socketChannel` is closed
   307	     * and its selection key cancelled.
   308	     * </p>
   309	     */
   310	    public void register(String id, SocketChannel socketChannel) throws IOException {
   311	        ensureNotRegistered(id);
   312	        registerChannel(id, socketChannel, SelectionKey.OP_READ);
   313	        this.sensors.connectionCreated.record();
   314	        // Default to empty client information as the ApiVersionsRequest is not
   315	        // mandatory. In this case, we still want to account for the connection.
   316	        ChannelMetadataRegistry metadataRegistry = this.channel(id).channelMetadataRegistry();
   317	        if (metadataRegistry.clientInformation() == null)
   318	            metadataRegistry.registerClientInformation(ClientInformation.EMPTY);
   319	    }

   321	    private void ensureNotRegistered(String id) {
   322	        if (this.channels.containsKey(id))
   323	            throw new IllegalStateException("There is already a connection for id " + id);
   324	        if (this.closingChannels.containsKey(id))
   325	            throw new IllegalStateException("There is already a connection for id " + id + " that is still being closed");
   326	    }

   328	    protected SelectionKey registerChannel(String id, SocketChannel socketChannel, int interestedOps) throws IOException {
   329	        SelectionKey key = socketChannel.register(nioSelector, interestedOps);
   330	        KafkaChannel channel = buildAndAttachKafkaChannel(socketChannel, id, key);
   331	        this.channels.put(id, channel);
   332	        if (idleExpiryManager != null)
   333	            idleExpiryManager.update(channel.id(), time.nanoseconds());
   334	        return key;
   335	    }
   336	
   337	    private KafkaChannel buildAndAttachKafkaChannel(SocketChannel socketChannel, String id, SelectionKey key) throws IOException {
   338	        ChannelMetadataRegistry metadataRegistry = null;
   339	        try {
   340	            metadataRegistry = new SelectorChannelMetadataRegistry();

   342	            key.attach(channel);
   343	            return channel;
   344	        } catch (Exception e) {
   345	            try {
   346	                socketChannel.close();
   347	            } finally {

   351	            // error, this builder should close the resources it has created instead.

   353	            throw new IOException("Channel could not be created for socket " + socketChannel, e);
   354	        }

   368	    @Override
   369	    public void close() {
   370	        List<String> connections = new ArrayList<>(channels.keySet());
   371	        AtomicReference<Throwable> firstException = new AtomicReference<>();
   372	        Utils.closeAllQuietly(firstException, "release connections",
   373	                connections.stream().map(id -> (AutoCloseable) () -> close(id)).toArray(AutoCloseable[]::new));
   374	        // If there is any exception thrown in close(id), we should still be able
   375	        // to close the remaining objects, especially the sensors because keeping
   376	        // the sensors may lead to failure to start up the ReplicaFetcherThread if
   377	        // the old sensors with the same names has not yet been cleaned up.
   378	        Utils.closeQuietly(nioSelector, "nioSelector", firstException);
   379	        Utils.closeQuietly(sensors, "sensors", firstException);
   380	        Utils.closeQuietly(channelBuilder, "channelBuilder", firstException);
   381	        Throwable exception = firstException.get();
   382	        if (exception instanceof RuntimeException && !(exception instanceof SecurityException)) {
   383	            throw (RuntimeException) exception;
   384	        }

   387	    /**
   388	     * Queue the given request for sending in the subsequent {@link #poll(long)} calls
   389	     * @param send The request to send
   390	     */
   391	    public void send(NetworkSend send) {
   392	        String connectionId = send.destinationId();
   393	        KafkaChannel channel = openOrClosingChannelOrFail(connectionId);
   394	        if (closingChannels.containsKey(connectionId)) {
   395	            // ensure notification via `disconnected`, leave channel in the state in which closing was triggered
   396	            this.failedSends.add(connectionId);
   397	        } else {
   398	            try {
   399	                channel.setSend(send);
   400	            } catch (Exception e) {
   401	                // update the state for consistency, the channel will be discarded after `close`
   402	                channel.state(ChannelState.FAILED_SEND);
   403	                // ensure notification via `disconnected` when `failedSends` are processed in the next poll
   404	                this.failedSends.add(connectionId);
   405	                close(channel, CloseMode.DISCARD_NO_NOTIFY);
   406	                if (!(e instanceof CancelledKeyException)) {
   407	                    log.error("Unexpected exception during send, closing connection {} and rethrowing exception.",
   408	                            connectionId, e);
   409	                    throw e;
   410	                }
   411	            }
   412	        }
   413	    }

   415	    /**
   416	     * Do whatever I/O can be done on each connection without blocking. This includes completing connections, completing
   417	     * disconnections, initiating new sends, or making progress on in-progress sends or receives.
   418	     *
   419	     * When this call is completed the user can check for completed sends, receives, connections or disconnects using
   420	     * {@link #completedSends()}, {@link #completedReceives()}, {@link #connected()}, {@link #disconnected()}. These
   421	     * lists will be cleared at the beginning of each `poll` call and repopulated by the call if there is
   422	     * any completed I/O.
   423	     *
   424	     * In the "Plaintext" setting, we are using socketChannel to read & write to the network. But for the "SSL" setting,
   425	     * we encrypt the data before we use socketChannel to write data to the network, and decrypt before we return the responses.
   426	     * This requires additional buffers to be maintained as we are reading from network, since the data on the wire is encrypted
   427	     * we won't be able to read exact no.of bytes as kafka protocol requires. We read as many bytes as we can, up to SSLEngine's
   428	     * application buffer size. This means we might be reading additional bytes than the requested size.
   429	     * If there is no further data to read from socketChannel selector won't invoke that channel and we have additional bytes
   430	     * in the buffer. To overcome this issue we added "keysWithBufferedRead" map which tracks channels which have data in the SSL

   430	     * in the buffer. To overcome this issue we added "keysWithBufferedRead" map which tracks channels which have data in the SSL
   431	     * buffers. If there are channels with buffered data that can by processed, we set "timeout" to 0 and process the data even
   432	     * if there is no more data to read from the socket.
   433	     *
   434	     * At most one entry is added to "completedReceives" for a channel in each poll. This is necessary to guarantee that
   435	     * requests from a channel are processed on the broker in the order they are sent. Since outstanding requests added
   436	     * by SocketServer to the request queue may be processed by different request handler threads, requests on each
   437	     * channel must be processed one-at-a-time to guarantee ordering.
   438	     *
   439	     * @param timeout The amount of time to wait, in milliseconds, which must be non-negative
   440	     * @throws IllegalArgumentException If `timeout` is negative
   441	     * @throws IllegalStateException If a send is given for which we have no existing connection or for which there is
   442	     *         already an in-progress send
   443	     */
   444	    @Override
   445	    public void poll(long timeout) throws IOException {
   446	        if (timeout < 0)
   447	            throw new IllegalArgumentException("timeout should be >= 0");
   448	
   449	        boolean madeReadProgressLastCall = madeReadProgressLastPoll;
   450	        clear();
   451	
   452	        boolean dataInBuffers = !keysWithBufferedRead.isEmpty();
   453	
   454	        if (!immediatelyConnectedKeys.isEmpty() || (madeReadProgressLastCall && dataInBuffers))
   455	            timeout = 0;
   456	
   457	        if (!memoryPool.isOutOfMemory() && outOfMemory) {
   458	            //we have recovered from memory pressure. unmute any channel not explicitly muted for other reasons
   459	            log.trace("Broker no longer low on memory - unmuting incoming sockets");
   460	            for (KafkaChannel channel : channels.values()) {
   461	                if (channel.isInMutableState() && !explicitlyMutedChannels.contains(channel)) {
   462	                    channel.maybeUnmute();
   463	                }
   464	            }
   465	            outOfMemory = false;
   466	        }
   467	
   468	        /* check ready keys */
   469	        long startSelect = time.nanoseconds();
   470	        int numReadyKeys = select(timeout);
   471	        long endSelect = time.nanoseconds();
   472	        this.sensors.selectTime.record(endSelect - startSelect, time.milliseconds(), false);
   473	
   474	        if (numReadyKeys > 0 || !immediatelyConnectedKeys.isEmpty() || dataInBuffers) {
   475	            Set<SelectionKey> readyKeys = this.nioSelector.selectedKeys();
   476	
   477	            // Poll from channels that have buffered data (but nothing more from the underlying socket)
   478	            if (dataInBuffers) {
   479	                keysWithBufferedRead.removeAll(readyKeys); //so no channel gets polled twice
   480	                Set<SelectionKey> toPoll = keysWithBufferedRead;
   481	                keysWithBufferedRead = new HashSet<>(); //poll() calls will repopulate if needed
   482	                pollSelectionKeys(toPoll, false, endSelect);
   483	            }
   484	
   485	            // Poll from channels where the underlying socket has more data
   486	            pollSelectionKeys(readyKeys, false, endSelect);
   487	            // Clear all selected keys so that they are excluded from the ready count for the next select
   488	            readyKeys.clear();
   489	
   490	            pollSelectionKeys(immediatelyConnectedKeys, true, endSelect);
   491	            immediatelyConnectedKeys.clear();
   492	        } else {
   493	            madeReadProgressLastPoll = true; //no work is also "progress"
   494	        }
   495	
   496	        long endIo = time.nanoseconds();
   497	        this.sensors.ioTime.record(endIo - endSelect, time.milliseconds(), false);
   498	
   499	        // Close channels that were delayed and are now ready to be closed
   500	        completeDelayedChannelClose(endIo);
   501	
   502	        // we use the time at the end of select to ensure that we don't close any connections that
   503	        // have just been processed in pollSelectionKeys
   504	        maybeCloseOldestConnection(endSelect);
   505	    }
   506	
   507	    /**
   508	     * handle any ready I/O on a set of selection keys
   509	     * @param selectionKeys set of keys to handle
   510	     * @param isImmediatelyConnected true if running over a set of keys for just-connected sockets
   511	     * @param currentTimeNanos time at which set of keys was determined

   513	    // package-private for testing
   514	    void pollSelectionKeys(Set<SelectionKey> selectionKeys,
   515	                           boolean isImmediatelyConnected,
   516	                           long currentTimeNanos) {
   517	        for (SelectionKey key : determineHandlingOrder(selectionKeys)) {
   518	            KafkaChannel channel = channel(key);
   519	            long channelStartTimeNanos = recordTimePerConnection ? time.nanoseconds() : 0;
   520	            boolean sendFailed = false;
   521	            String nodeId = channel.id();
   522	
   523	            // register all per-connection metrics at once
   524	            sensors.maybeRegisterConnectionMetrics(nodeId);
   525	            if (idleExpiryManager != null)
   526	                idleExpiryManager.update(nodeId, currentTimeNanos);
   527	
   528	            try {
   529	                /* complete any connections that have finished their handshake (either normally or immediately) */
   530	                if (isImmediatelyConnected || key.isConnectable()) {
   531	                    if (channel.finishConnect()) {
   532	                        this.connected.add(nodeId);
   533	                        this.sensors.connectionCreated.record();

   537	                                socketChannel.socket().getReceiveBufferSize(),
   538	                                socketChannel.socket().getSendBufferSize(),
   539	                                socketChannel.socket().getSoTimeout(),
   540	                                nodeId);
   541	                    } else {
   542	                        continue;
   543	                    }
   544	                }
   545	
   546	                /* if channel is not ready finish prepare */
   547	                if (channel.isConnected() && !channel.ready()) {
   548	                    channel.prepare();
   549	                    if (channel.ready()) {
   550	                        long readyTimeMs = time.milliseconds();
   551	                        boolean isReauthentication = channel.successfulAuthentications() > 1;
   552	                        if (isReauthentication) {
   553	                            sensors.successfulReauthentication.record(1.0, readyTimeMs);
   554	                            channel.maybeAddWriteInterestAfterReauth();
   555	                            if (channel.reauthenticationLatencyMs() == null)

   558	                            else

   561	                        } else {
   562	                            sensors.successfulAuthentication.record(1.0, readyTimeMs);
   563	                            if (!channel.connectedClientSupportsReauthentication())
   564	                                sensors.successfulAuthenticationNoReauth.record(1.0, readyTimeMs);
   565	                        }
   566	                        log.debug("Successfully {}authenticated with {}", isReauthentication ?
   567	                            "re-" : "", channel.socketDescription());
   568	                    }
   569	                }

   574	                    long currentTimeMs = time.milliseconds();
   575	                    addToCompletedReceives(channel, receive, currentTimeMs);
   576	                });

   578	                //if channel is ready and has bytes to read from socket or buffer, and has no
   579	                //previous completed receive then read from it
   580	                if (channel.ready() && (key.isReadable() || channel.hasBytesBuffered()) && !hasCompletedReceive(channel)

   582	                    attemptRead(channel);
   583	                }
   584	
   585	                if (channel.hasBytesBuffered() && !explicitlyMutedChannels.contains(channel)) {
   586	                    //this channel has bytes enqueued in intermediary buffers that we could not read
   587	                    //(possibly because no memory). it may be the case that the underlying socket will
   588	                    //not come up in the next poll() and so we need to remember this channel for the
   589	                    //next poll call otherwise data may be stuck in said buffers forever. If we attempt
   590	                    //to process buffered data and no progress is made, the channel buffered status is
   591	                    //cleared to avoid the overhead of checking every time.
   592	                    keysWithBufferedRead.add(key);
   593	                }
   594	
   595	                /* if channel is ready write to any sockets that have space in their buffer and for which we have data */
   596	
   597	                long nowNanos = channelStartTimeNanos != 0 ? channelStartTimeNanos : currentTimeNanos;
   598	                try {
   599	                    attemptWrite(key, channel, nowNanos);
   600	                } catch (Exception e) {
   601	                    sendFailed = true;
   602	                    throw e;
   603	                }
   604	
   605	                /* cancel any defunct sockets */
   606	                if (!key.isValid())
   607	                    close(channel, CloseMode.GRACEFUL);
   608	
   609	            } catch (Exception e) {
   610	                String desc = String.format("%s (channelId=%s)", channel.socketDescription(), channel.id());
   611	                if (e instanceof IOException) {
   612	                    log.debug("Connection with {} disconnected", desc, e);
   613	                } else if (e instanceof AuthenticationException) {
   614	                    boolean isReauthentication = channel.successfulAuthentications() > 0;
   615	                    if (isReauthentication)
   616	                        sensors.failedReauthentication.record();
   617	                    else
   618	                        sensors.failedAuthentication.record();
   619	                    String exceptionMessage = e.getMessage();
   620	                    if (e instanceof DelayedResponseAuthenticationException)

   638	    private void attemptWrite(SelectionKey key, KafkaChannel channel, long nowNanos) throws IOException {
   639	        if (channel.hasSend()
   640	                && channel.ready()
   641	                && key.isWritable()
   642	                && !channel.maybeBeginClientReauthentication(() -> nowNanos)) {
   643	            write(channel);
   644	        }
   645	    }

   647	    // package-private for testing
   648	    void write(KafkaChannel channel) throws IOException {
   649	        String nodeId = channel.id();
   650	        long bytesSent = channel.write();
   651	        NetworkSend send = channel.maybeCompleteSend();
   652	        // We may complete the send with bytesSent < 1 if `TransportLayer.hasPendingWrites` was true and `channel.write()`
   653	        // caused the pending writes to be written to the socket channel buffer
   654	        if (bytesSent > 0 || send != null) {
   655	            long currentTimeMs = time.milliseconds();
   656	            if (bytesSent > 0)
   657	                this.sensors.recordBytesSent(nodeId, bytesSent, currentTimeMs);
   658	            if (send != null) {
   659	                this.completedSends.add(send);
   660	                this.sensors.recordCompletedSend(nodeId, send.size(), currentTimeMs);
   661	            }
   662	        }
   663	    }
   664	
   665	    private Collection<SelectionKey> determineHandlingOrder(Set<SelectionKey> selectionKeys) {
   666	        //it is possible that the iteration order over selectionKeys is the same every invocation.
   667	        //this may cause starvation of reads when memory is low. to address this we shuffle the keys if memory is low.
   668	        if (!outOfMemory && memoryPool.availableMemory() < lowMemThreshold) {
   669	            List<SelectionKey> shuffledKeys = new ArrayList<>(selectionKeys);
   670	            Collections.shuffle(shuffledKeys);
   671	            return shuffledKeys;
   672	        } else {
   673	            return selectionKeys;
   674	        }
   675	    }
   676	
   677	    private void attemptRead(KafkaChannel channel) throws IOException {
   678	        String nodeId = channel.id();
   679	
   680	        long bytesReceived = channel.read();
   681	        if (bytesReceived != 0) {
   682	            long currentTimeMs = time.milliseconds();
   683	            sensors.recordBytesReceived(nodeId, bytesReceived, currentTimeMs);
   684	            madeReadProgressLastPoll = true;
   685	
   686	            NetworkReceive receive = channel.maybeCompleteReceive();
   687	            if (receive != null) {
   688	                addToCompletedReceives(channel, receive, currentTimeMs);
   689	            }
   690	        }
   691	        if (channel.isMuted()) {
   692	            outOfMemory = true; //channel has muted itself due to memory pressure.
   693	        } else {
   694	            madeReadProgressLastPoll = true;
   695	        }
   696	    }
   697	
   698	    private boolean maybeReadFromClosingChannel(KafkaChannel channel) {
   699	        boolean hasPending;
   700	        if (channel.state().state() != ChannelState.State.READY)
   701	            hasPending = false;
   702	        else if (explicitlyMutedChannels.contains(channel) || hasCompletedReceive(channel))
   703	            hasPending = true;
   704	        else {
   705	            try {
   706	                attemptRead(channel);
   707	                hasPending = hasCompletedReceive(channel);
   708	            } catch (Exception e) {
   709	                log.trace("Read from closing channel failed, ignoring exception", e);
   710	                hasPending = false;
   711	            }
   712	        }
   713	        return hasPending;
   714	    }
   715	
   716	    // Record time spent in pollSelectionKeys for channel (moved into a method to keep checkstyle happy)
   717	    private void maybeRecordTimePerConnection(KafkaChannel channel, long startTimeNanos) {
   718	        if (recordTimePerConnection)
   719	            channel.addNetworkThreadTimeNanos(time.nanoseconds() - startTimeNanos);
   720	    }

   784	    void completeDelayedChannelClose(long currentTimeNanos) {
   785	        if (delayedClosingChannels == null)
   786	            return;
   787	
   788	        while (!delayedClosingChannels.isEmpty()) {
   789	            DelayedAuthenticationFailureClose delayedClose = delayedClosingChannels.values().iterator().next();
   790	            if (!delayedClose.tryClose(currentTimeNanos))
   791	                break;
   792	        }
   793	    }

   796	        if (idleExpiryManager == null)
   797	            return;
   798	
   799	        Map.Entry<String, Long> expiredConnection = idleExpiryManager.pollExpiredConnection(currentTimeNanos);
   800	        if (expiredConnection != null) {

   806	                            connectionId, (currentTimeNanos - expiredConnection.getValue()) / 1000 / 1000);

   831	    /**
   832	     * Clears all the results from the previous poll. This is invoked by Selector at the start of
   833	     * a poll() when all the results from the previous poll are expected to have been handled.
   834	     * <p>
   835	     * SocketServer uses {@link #clearCompletedSends()} and {@link #clearCompletedReceives()} to
   836	     * clear `completedSends` and `completedReceives` as soon as they are processed to avoid
   837	     * holding onto large request/response buffers from multiple connections longer than necessary.
   838	     * Clients rely on Selector invoking {@link #clear()} at the start of each poll() since memory usage
   839	     * is less critical and clearing once-per-poll provides the flexibility to process these results in
   840	     * any order before the next poll.
   841	     */
   842	    private void clear() {
   843	        this.completedSends.clear();
   844	        this.completedReceives.clear();
   845	        this.connected.clear();
   846	        this.disconnected.clear();
   847	
   848	        // Remove closed channels after all their buffered receives have been processed or if a send was requested
   849	        for (Iterator<Map.Entry<String, KafkaChannel>> it = closingChannels.entrySet().iterator(); it.hasNext(); ) {
   850	            KafkaChannel channel = it.next().getValue();
   851	            boolean sendFailed = failedSends.remove(channel.id());
   852	            boolean hasPending = false;
   853	            if (!sendFailed)
   854	                hasPending = maybeReadFromClosingChannel(channel);
   855	            if (!hasPending) {
   856	                doClose(channel, true);
   857	                it.remove();
   858	            }
   859	        }
   860	
   861	        for (String channel : this.failedSends)
   862	            this.disconnected.put(channel, ChannelState.FAILED_SEND);
   863	        this.failedSends.clear();
   864	        this.madeReadProgressLastPoll = false;
   865	    }

   867	    /**
   868	     * Check for data, waiting up to the given timeout.
   869	     *
   870	     * @param timeoutMs Length of time to wait, in milliseconds, which must be non-negative
   871	     * @return The number of keys ready
   872	     */
   873	    private int select(long timeoutMs) throws IOException {
   874	        if (timeoutMs < 0L)
   875	            throw new IllegalArgumentException("timeout should be >= 0");
   876	
   877	        if (timeoutMs == 0L)
   878	            return this.nioSelector.selectNow();
   879	        else
   880	            return this.nioSelector.select(timeoutMs);
   881	    }

   883	    /**
   884	     * Close the connection identified by the given id
   885	     */
   886	    public void close(String id) {
   887	        KafkaChannel channel = this.channels.get(id);
   888	        if (channel != null) {
   889	            // There is no disconnect notification for local close, but updating
   890	            // channel state here anyway to avoid confusion.
   891	            channel.state(ChannelState.LOCAL_CLOSE);
   892	            close(channel, CloseMode.DISCARD_NO_NOTIFY);
   893	        } else {
   894	            KafkaChannel closingChannel = this.closingChannels.remove(id);
   895	            // Close any closing channel, leave the channel in the state in which closing was triggered
   896	            if (closingChannel != null)
   897	                doClose(closingChannel, false);
   898	        }
   899	    }

   901	    private void maybeDelayCloseOnAuthenticationFailure(KafkaChannel channel) {
   902	        DelayedAuthenticationFailureClose delayedClose = new DelayedAuthenticationFailureClose(channel, failedAuthenticationDelayMs);
   903	        if (delayedClosingChannels != null)
   904	            delayedClosingChannels.put(channel.id(), delayedClose);
   905	        else
   906	            delayedClose.closeNow();
   907	    }

   909	    private void handleCloseOnAuthenticationFailure(KafkaChannel channel) {
   910	        try {
   911	            channel.completeCloseOnAuthenticationFailure();
   912	        } catch (Exception e) {
   913	            log.error("Exception handling close on authentication failure node {}", channel.id(), e);
   914	        } finally {
   915	            close(channel, CloseMode.GRACEFUL);
   916	        }
   917	    }

   920	     * Begin closing this connection.
   921	     * If 'closeMode' is `CloseMode.GRACEFUL`, the channel is disconnected here, but outstanding receives
   922	     * are processed. The channel is closed when there are no outstanding receives or if a send is
   923	     * requested. For other values of `closeMode`, outstanding receives are discarded and the channel
   924	     * is closed immediately.
   925	     *
   926	     * The channel will be added to disconnect list when it is actually closed if `closeMode.notifyDisconnect`
   927	     * is true.
   928	     */
   929	    private void close(KafkaChannel channel, CloseMode closeMode) {
   930	        channel.disconnect();
   931	
   932	        // Ensure that `connected` does not have closed channels. This could happen if `prepare` throws an exception
   933	        // in the `poll` invocation when `finishConnect` succeeds
   934	        connected.remove(channel.id());
   935	
   936	        // Keep track of closed channels with pending receives so that all received records
   937	        // may be processed. For example, when producer with acks=0 sends some records and
   938	        // closes its connections, a single poll() in the broker may receive records and
   939	        // handle close(). When the remote end closes its connection, the channel is retained until
   940	        // a send fails or all outstanding receives are processed. Mute state of disconnected channels
   941	        // are tracked to ensure that requests are processed one-by-one by the broker to preserve ordering.
   942	        if (closeMode == CloseMode.GRACEFUL && maybeReadFromClosingChannel(channel)) {
   943	            closingChannels.put(channel.id(), channel);
   944	            log.debug("Tracking closing connection {} to process outstanding requests", channel.id());
   945	        } else {
   946	            doClose(channel, closeMode.notifyDisconnect);
   947	        }
   948	        this.channels.remove(channel.id());
   949	
   950	        if (delayedClosingChannels != null)
   951	            delayedClosingChannels.remove(channel.id());
   952	
   953	        if (idleExpiryManager != null)
   954	            idleExpiryManager.remove(channel.id());
   955	    }
   956	
   957	    private void doClose(KafkaChannel channel, boolean notifyDisconnect) {
   958	        SelectionKey key = channel.selectionKey();
   959	        try {
   960	            immediatelyConnectedKeys.remove(key);
   961	            keysWithBufferedRead.remove(key);
   962	            channel.close();
   963	        } catch (IOException e) {
   964	            log.error("Exception closing connection to node {}:", channel.id(), e);
   965	        } finally {
   966	            key.cancel();
   967	            key.attach(null);
   968	        }
   969	
   970	        this.sensors.connectionClosed.record();
   971	        this.explicitlyMutedChannels.remove(channel);
   972	        if (notifyDisconnect)
   973	            this.disconnected.put(channel.id(), channel.state());
   974	    }

   985	    private KafkaChannel openOrClosingChannelOrFail(String id) {
   986	        KafkaChannel channel = this.channels.get(id);
   987	        if (channel == null)
   988	            channel = this.closingChannels.get(id);
   989	        if (channel == null)
   990	            throw new IllegalStateException("Attempt to retrieve channel for which there is no connection. Connection id " + id + " existing connections " + channels.keySet());
   991	        return channel;
   992	    }

  1017	    /**
  1018	     * Returns the lowest priority channel chosen using the following sequence:
  1019	     *   1) If one or more channels are in closing state, return any one of them
  1020	     *   2) If idle expiry manager is enabled, return the least recently updated channel
  1021	     *   3) Otherwise return any of the channels
  1022	     *
  1023	     * This method is used to close a channel to accommodate a new channel on the inter-broker listener
  1024	     * when broker-wide `max.connections` limit is enabled.
  1025	     */
  1026	    public KafkaChannel lowestPriorityChannel() {
  1027	        KafkaChannel channel = null;
  1028	        if (!closingChannels.isEmpty()) {
  1029	            channel = closingChannels.values().iterator().next();
  1030	        } else if (idleExpiryManager != null && !idleExpiryManager.lruConnections.isEmpty()) {
  1031	            String channelId = idleExpiryManager.lruConnections.keySet().iterator().next();
  1032	            channel = channel(channelId);
  1033	        } else if (!channels.isEmpty()) {
  1034	            channel = channels.values().iterator().next();
  1035	        }
  1036	        return channel;
  1037	    }
  1038	
  1039	    /**
  1040	     * Get the channel associated with selectionKey
  1041	     */
  1042	    private KafkaChannel channel(SelectionKey key) {
  1043	        return (KafkaChannel) key.attachment();
  1044	    }
  1045	
  1046	    /**
  1047	     * Check if given channel has a completed receive
  1048	     */
  1049	    private boolean hasCompletedReceive(KafkaChannel channel) {
  1050	        return completedReceives.containsKey(channel.id());
  1051	    }
  1052	
  1053	    /**
  1054	     * adds a receive to completed receives
  1055	     */
  1056	    private void addToCompletedReceives(KafkaChannel channel, NetworkReceive networkReceive, long currentTimeMs) {
  1057	        if (hasCompletedReceive(channel))
  1058	            throw new IllegalStateException("Attempting to add second completed receive to channel " + channel.id());
  1059	
  1060	        this.completedReceives.put(channel.id(), networkReceive);
  1061	        sensors.recordCompletedReceive(channel.id(), networkReceive.size(), currentTimeMs);
  1062	    }

  1077	                if (this.cipherInformation.equals(cipherInformation))
  1078	                    return;
  1079	                sensors.connectionsByCipher.decrement(this.cipherInformation);
  1080	            }
  1081	
  1082	            this.cipherInformation = cipherInformation;
  1083	            sensors.connectionsByCipher.increment(cipherInformation);
  1084	        }

  1422	            closed = true;
  1423	        }
  1424	    }
  1425	
  1426	    // helper class for tracking least recently used connections to enable idle connection closing
  1427	    private static class IdleExpiryManager {
  1428	        private final Map<String, Long> lruConnections;
  1429	        private final long connectionsMaxIdleNanos;
  1430	        private long nextIdleCloseCheckTime;
  1431	
  1432	        public IdleExpiryManager(Time time, long connectionsMaxIdleMs) {
  1433	            this.connectionsMaxIdleNanos = connectionsMaxIdleMs * 1000 * 1000;
  1434	            // initial capacity and load factor are default, we set them explicitly because we want to set accessOrder = true
  1435	            this.lruConnections = new LinkedHashMap<>(16, .75F, true);
  1436	            this.nextIdleCloseCheckTime = time.nanoseconds() + this.connectionsMaxIdleNanos;
  1437	        }
  1438	
  1439	        public void update(String connectionId, long currentTimeNanos) {
  1440	            lruConnections.put(connectionId, currentTimeNanos);
  1441	        }
  1442	
  1443	        public Map.Entry<String, Long> pollExpiredConnection(long currentTimeNanos) {
  1444	            if (currentTimeNanos <= nextIdleCloseCheckTime)
  1445	                return null;
  1446	
  1447	            if (lruConnections.isEmpty()) {
  1448	                nextIdleCloseCheckTime = currentTimeNanos + connectionsMaxIdleNanos;
  1449	                return null;
  1450	            }
  1451	
  1452	            Map.Entry<String, Long> oldestConnectionEntry = lruConnections.entrySet().iterator().next();
  1453	            Long connectionLastActiveTime = oldestConnectionEntry.getValue();
  1454	            nextIdleCloseCheckTime = connectionLastActiveTime + connectionsMaxIdleNanos;
  1455	
  1456	            if (currentTimeNanos > nextIdleCloseCheckTime)
  1457	                return oldestConnectionEntry;
  1458	            else
  1459	                return null;
  1460	        }
  1461	
  1462	        public void remove(String connectionId) {
  1463	            lruConnections.remove(connectionId);
  1464	        }
  1465	    }
  1466	
  1467	    //package-private for testing
  1468	    boolean isOutOfMemory() {
  1469	        return outOfMemory;
  1470	    }
  1471	
  1472	    //package-private for testing
  1473	    boolean isMadeReadProgressLastPoll() {
  1474	        return madeReadProgressLastPoll;
  1475	    }
  1476	
  1477	    // package-private for testing
  1478	    Map<?, ?> delayedClosingChannels() {
  1479	        return delayedClosingChannels;
  1480	    }
  1481	}

    39	     * Constructs a plaintext channel builder. ListenerName is non-null whenever

    42	    public PlaintextChannelBuilder(ListenerName listenerName) {

    44	    }

    47	        this.configs = configs;

    50	    @Override
    51	    public KafkaChannel buildChannel(String id, SelectionKey key, int maxReceiveSize,
    52	                                     MemoryPool memoryPool, ChannelMetadataRegistry metadataRegistry) throws KafkaException {
    53	        PlaintextTransportLayer transportLayer = null;
    54	        try {
    55	            transportLayer = buildTransportLayer(key);
    56	            final PlaintextTransportLayer finalTransportLayer = transportLayer;
    57	            Supplier<Authenticator> authenticatorCreator = () -> new PlaintextAuthenticator(configs, finalTransportLayer, listenerName);
    58	            return buildChannel(id, transportLayer, authenticatorCreator, maxReceiveSize,
    59	                    memoryPool != null ? memoryPool : MemoryPool.NONE, metadataRegistry);
    60	        } catch (Exception e) {
    61	            // Ideally these resources are closed by the KafkaChannel but this builder should close the resources instead
    62	            // if an error occurs due to which KafkaChannel is not created.
    63	            Utils.closeQuietly(transportLayer, "transport layer for channel Id: " + id);
    64	            throw new KafkaException(e);
    65	        }
    66	    }
    67

    69	    KafkaChannel buildChannel(String id, TransportLayer transportLayer, Supplier<Authenticator> authenticatorCreator,
    70	                              int maxReceiveSize, MemoryPool memoryPool, ChannelMetadataRegistry metadataRegistry) {
    71	        return new KafkaChannel(id, transportLayer, authenticatorCreator, maxReceiveSize, memoryPool, metadataRegistry);
    72	    }

    87	            this.transportLayer = transportLayer;
    88	            this.principalBuilder = ChannelBuilders.createPrincipalBuilder(configs, null, null);

    95	        @Override
    96	        public KafkaPrincipal principal() {
    97	            InetAddress clientAddress = transportLayer.socketChannel().socket().getInetAddress();
    98	            // listenerName should only be null in Client mode where principal() should not be called
    99	            if (listenerName == null)
   100	                throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected call to principal() when listenerName is null");
   101	            return principalBuilder.build(new PlaintextAuthenticationContext(clientAddress, listenerName.value()));
   102	        }

   106	            return principalBuilder instanceof KafkaPrincipalSerde ? Optional.of((KafkaPrincipalSerde) principalBuilder) : Optional.empty();
   107	        }

   112	        }

   117	                Utils.closeQuietly((Closeable) principalBuilder, "principal builder");
   118	        }

 succeeded in 0ms:
   170	     * Does handshake of transportLayer and authentication using configured authenticator.
   171	     * For SSL with client authentication enabled, {@link TransportLayer#handshake()} performs
   172	     * authentication. For SASL, authentication is performed by {@link Authenticator#authenticate()}.
   173	     */
   174	    public void prepare() throws AuthenticationException, IOException {
   175	        boolean authenticating = false;
   176	        try {
   177	            if (!transportLayer.ready())
   178	                transportLayer.handshake();
   179	            if (transportLayer.ready() && !authenticator.complete()) {
   180	                authenticating = true;
   181	                authenticator.authenticate();
   182	            }
   183	        } catch (AuthenticationException e) {
   184	            // Clients are notified of authentication exceptions to enable operations to be terminated
   185	            // without retries. Other errors are handled as network exceptions in Selector.
   186	            String remoteDesc = remoteAddress != null ? remoteAddress.toString() : null;
   187	            state = new ChannelState(ChannelState.State.AUTHENTICATION_FAILED, e, remoteDesc);
   188	            if (authenticating) {
   189	                delayCloseOnAuthenticationFailure();
   190	                throw new DelayedResponseAuthenticationException(e);
   191	            }
   192	            throw e;
   193	        }
   194	        if (ready()) {
   195	            ++successfulAuthentications;
   196	            state = ChannelState.READY;
   197	        }
   198	    }
   199	
   200	    public void disconnect() {
   201	        disconnected = true;
   202	        if (state == ChannelState.NOT_CONNECTED && remoteAddress != null) {
   203	            //if we captured the remote address we can provide more information
   204	            state = new ChannelState(ChannelState.State.NOT_CONNECTED, remoteAddress.toString());
   205	        }
   206	        transportLayer.disconnect();
   207	    }
   208

   217	    public boolean finishConnect() throws IOException {
   218	        //we need to grab remoteAddr before finishConnect() is called otherwise
   219	        //it becomes inaccessible if the connection was refused.
   220	        SocketChannel socketChannel = transportLayer.socketChannel();
   221	        if (socketChannel != null) {
   222	            remoteAddress = socketChannel.getRemoteAddress();
   223	        }
   224	        boolean connected = transportLayer.finishConnect();
   225	        if (connected) {
   226	            if (ready()) {
   227	                state = ChannelState.READY;
   228	            } else if (remoteAddress != null) {
   229	                state = new ChannelState(ChannelState.State.AUTHENTICATE, remoteAddress.toString());
   230	            } else {
   231	                state = ChannelState.AUTHENTICATE;
   232	            }
   233	        }
   234	        return connected;
   235	    }

   249	    /**
   250	     * externally muting a channel should be done via selector to ensure proper state handling
   251	     */
   252	    void mute() {
   253	        if (muteState == ChannelMuteState.NOT_MUTED) {
   254	            if (!disconnected) transportLayer.removeInterestOps(SelectionKey.OP_READ);
   255	            muteState = ChannelMuteState.MUTED;
   256	        }
   257	    }
   258	
   259	    /**
   260	     * Unmute the channel. The channel can be unmuted only if it is in the MUTED state. For other muted states
   261	     * (MUTED_AND_*), this is a no-op.
   262	     *
   263	     * @return Whether or not the channel is in the NOT_MUTED state after the call
   264	     */
   265	    boolean maybeUnmute() {
   266	        if (muteState == ChannelMuteState.MUTED) {
   267	            if (!disconnected) transportLayer.addInterestOps(SelectionKey.OP_READ);
   268	            muteState = ChannelMuteState.NOT_MUTED;
   269	        }
   270	        return muteState == ChannelMuteState.NOT_MUTED;
   271	    }
   272	
   273	    // Handle the specified channel mute-related event and transition the mute state according to the state machine.
   274	    public void handleChannelMuteEvent(ChannelMuteEvent event) {
   275	        boolean stateChanged = false;
   276	        switch (event) {
   277	            case REQUEST_RECEIVED:
   278	                if (muteState == ChannelMuteState.MUTED) {
   279	                    muteState = ChannelMuteState.MUTED_AND_RESPONSE_PENDING;
   280	                    stateChanged = true;
   281	                }
   282	                break;
   283	            case RESPONSE_SENT:
   284	                if (muteState == ChannelMuteState.MUTED_AND_RESPONSE_PENDING) {
   285	                    muteState = ChannelMuteState.MUTED;
   286	                    stateChanged = true;
   287	                }
   288	                if (muteState == ChannelMuteState.MUTED_AND_THROTTLED_AND_RESPONSE_PENDING) {
   289	                    muteState = ChannelMuteState.MUTED_AND_THROTTLED;
   290	                    stateChanged = true;
   291	                }
   292	                break;
   293	            case THROTTLE_STARTED:
   294	                if (muteState == ChannelMuteState.MUTED_AND_RESPONSE_PENDING) {
   295	                    muteState = ChannelMuteState.MUTED_AND_THROTTLED_AND_RESPONSE_PENDING;
   296	                    stateChanged = true;
   297	                }
   298	                break;
   299	            case THROTTLE_ENDED:
   300	                if (muteState == ChannelMuteState.MUTED_AND_THROTTLED) {
   301	                    muteState = ChannelMuteState.MUTED;
   302	                    stateChanged = true;
   303	                }
   304	                if (muteState == ChannelMuteState.MUTED_AND_THROTTLED_AND_RESPONSE_PENDING) {
   305	                    muteState = ChannelMuteState.MUTED_AND_RESPONSE_PENDING;
   306	                    stateChanged = true;
   307	                }
   308	        }
   309	        if (!stateChanged) {
   310	            throw new IllegalStateException("Cannot transition from " + muteState.name() + " for " + event.name());
   311	        }
   312	    }
   313	
   314	    public ChannelMuteState muteState() {
   315	        return muteState;
   316	    }

   318	    /**
   319	     * Delay channel close on authentication failure. This will remove all read/write operations from the channel until
   320	     * {@link #completeCloseOnAuthenticationFailure()} is called to finish up the channel close.
   321	     */
   322	    private void delayCloseOnAuthenticationFailure() {
   323	        transportLayer.removeInterestOps(SelectionKey.OP_WRITE);
   324	    }

   327	     * Finish up any processing on {@link #prepare()} failure.
   328	     * @throws IOException
   329	     */
   330	    void completeCloseOnAuthenticationFailure() throws IOException {
   331	        transportLayer.addInterestOps(SelectionKey.OP_WRITE);
   332	        // Invoke the underlying handler to finish up any processing on authentication failure
   333	        authenticator.handleAuthenticationFailure();
   334	    }

   343	    public boolean isInMutableState() {
   344	        // Some requests do not require memory, so if we do not know what the current (or future) request is
   345	        // (receive == null) we don't mute. We also don't mute if whatever memory required has already been
   346	        // successfully allocated (if none is required for the currently-being-read request
   347	        // receive.memoryAllocated() is expected to return true)
   348	        if (receive == null || receive.memoryAllocated())
   349	            return false;
   350	        // also cannot mute if underlying transport is not in the ready state
   351	        return transportLayer.ready();
   352	    }
   353	
   354	    public boolean ready() {
   355	        return transportLayer.ready() && authenticator.complete();
   356	    }

   389	    public void setSend(NetworkSend send) {

   391	            throw new IllegalStateException("Attempt to begin a send operation with prior send operation still in progress, connection id is " + id);
   392	        this.send = send;

   396	    public NetworkSend maybeCompleteSend() {
   397	        if (send != null && send.completed()) {
   398	            midWrite = false;
   399	            transportLayer.removeInterestOps(SelectionKey.OP_WRITE);
   400	            NetworkSend result = send;
   401	            send = null;
   402	            return result;
   403	        }
   404	        return null;
   405	    }

   407	    public long read() throws IOException {
   408	        if (receive == null) {
   409	            receive = new NetworkReceive(maxReceiveSize, id, memoryPool);
   410	        }
   411	
   412	        long bytesReceived = receive(this.receive);
   413	
   414	        if (this.receive.requiredMemoryAmountKnown() && !this.receive.memoryAllocated() && isInMutableState()) {
   415	            //pool must be out of memory, mute ourselves.
   416	            mute();
   417	        }
   418	        return bytesReceived;
   419	    }

   460	    private long receive(NetworkReceive receive) throws IOException {
   461	        try {
   462	            return receive.readFrom(transportLayer);
   463	        } catch (SslAuthenticationException e) {
   464	            // With TLSv1.3, post-handshake messages may throw SSLExceptions, which are
   465	            // handled as authentication failures
   466	            String remoteDesc = remoteAddress != null ? remoteAddress.toString() : null;
   467	            state = new ChannelState(ChannelState.State.AUTHENTICATION_FAILED, e, remoteDesc);
   468	            throw e;
   469	        }
   470	    }

    72	    @Override
    73	    public String source() {
    74	        return source;
    75	    }
    76	
    77	    @Override
    78	    public boolean complete() {
    79	        return !size.hasRemaining() && buffer != null && !buffer.hasRemaining();
    80	    }
    81	
    82	    public long readFrom(ScatteringByteChannel channel) throws IOException {
    83	        int read = 0;
    84	        if (size.hasRemaining()) {
    85	            int bytesRead = channel.read(size);
    86	            if (bytesRead < 0)
    87	                throw new EOFException();
    88	            read += bytesRead;
    89	            if (!size.hasRemaining()) {
    90	                size.rewind();
    91	                int receiveSize = size.getInt();
    92	                if (receiveSize < 0)
    93	                    throw new InvalidReceiveException("Invalid receive (size = " + receiveSize + ")");
    94	                if (maxSize != UNLIMITED && receiveSize > maxSize)
    95	                    throw new InvalidReceiveException("Invalid receive (size = " + receiveSize + " larger than " + maxSize + ")");
    96	                requestedBufferSize = receiveSize; // may be 0 for some payloads (SASL)
    97	                if (receiveSize == 0) {
    98	                    buffer = EMPTY_BUFFER;
    99	                }
   100	            }
   101	        }
   102	        if (buffer == null && requestedBufferSize != -1) { // we know the size we want but haven't been able to allocate it yet
   103	            buffer = memoryPool.tryAllocate(requestedBufferSize);
   104	            if (buffer == null)
   105	                log.trace("Broker low on memory - could not allocate buffer of size {} for source {}", requestedBufferSize, source);
   106	        }
   107	        if (buffer != null) {
   108	            int bytesRead = channel.read(buffer);
   109	            if (bytesRead < 0)
   110	                throw new EOFException();
   111	            read += bytesRead;
   112	        }
   113	
   114	        return read;
   115	    }
   116	
   117	    @Override
   118	    public boolean requiredMemoryAmountKnown() {
   119	        return requestedBufferSize != -1;
   120	    }
   121	
   122	    @Override
   123	    public boolean memoryAllocated() {
   124	        return buffer != null;
   125	    }
   126	
   127	
   128	    @Override
   129	    public void close() throws IOException {
   130	        if (buffer != null && buffer != EMPTY_BUFFER) {
   131	            memoryPool.release(buffer);
   132	            buffer = null;
   133	        }
   134	    }
   135	
   136	    public ByteBuffer payload() {
   137	        return this.buffer;
   138	    }
   139	
   140	    public int bytesRead() {
   141	        if (buffer == null)
   142	            return size.position();
   143	        return buffer.position() + size.position();
   144	    }

    46	    @Override
    47	    public boolean completed() {
    48	        return remaining <= 0 && !pending;
    49	    }
    50	
    51	    @Override
    52	    public long size() {
    53	        return this.size;
    54	    }

    56	    @Override
    57	    public long writeTo(TransferableChannel channel) throws IOException {
    58	        long written = channel.write(buffers);
    59	        if (written < 0)
    60	            throw new EOFException("Wrote negative bytes to channel. This shouldn't happen.");
    61	        remaining -= written;
    62	        pending = channel.hasPendingWrites();
    63	        return written;
    64	    }
    65	
    66	    public long remaining() {
    67	        return remaining;
    68	    }

    72	        return "ByteBufferSend(" +
    73	            ", size=" + size +
    74	            ", remaining=" + remaining +
    75	            ", pending=" + pending +
    76	            ')';
    77	    }

    81	        sizeBuffer.putInt(0, buffer.remaining());
    82	        return new ByteBufferSend(sizeBuffer, buffer);

    29	/**
    30	 * The minimum {@link SocketChannel} stub needed to wrap a Netty {@code IoUringSocketChannel}
    31	 * inside Kafka's {@code KafkaChannel}.
    32	 *
    33	 * <p>{@code KafkaChannel} (which we cannot modify — it lives in {@code clients/}) reads from
    34	 * its {@code transportLayer.socketChannel()} in exactly four places:
    35	 *
    36	 * <ul>
    37	 *   <li>{@code finishConnect()} — {@code socketChannel().getRemoteAddress()}</li>
    38	 *   <li>{@code socketAddress()} — {@code socketChannel().socket().getInetAddress()}</li>
    39	 *   <li>{@code socketPort()} — {@code socketChannel().socket().getPort()}</li>
    40	 *   <li>{@code socketDescription()} — {@code socketChannel().socket().getInetAddress()} +
    41	 *       {@code getLocalAddress()}</li>
    42	 * </ul>
    43	 *
    44	 * <p>All four read addresses that were captured when the Netty channel was accepted. This
    45	 * stub serves only those reads. Every other {@code SocketChannel} method throws
    46	 * {@link UnsupportedOperationException} so that any unexpected call site fails loudly
    47	 * rather than silently returning wrong data.
    48	 *
    49	 * <p>The stub deliberately does <em>not</em> register itself with a JDK {@code Selector};
    50	 * the io_uring backend drives I/O autonomously and never goes through {@code SelectorProvider}.
    51	 */
    52	final class StubSocketChannel extends SocketChannel {
    53

    55	    private final SocketAddress local;

    60	        this.remote = remote;
    61	        this.local = local;

    63	    }

    17	package org.apache.kafka.network.iouring;

    19	import java.nio.channels.SelectableChannel;
    20	import java.nio.channels.SelectionKey;
    21	import java.nio.channels.Selector;
    22	
    23	/**
    24	 * Inert {@link SelectionKey} that {@code IoUringTransportLayer.selectionKey()} returns.
    25	 *
    26	 * <p>The io_uring backend never registers a {@link SelectableChannel} with a JDK
    27	 * {@link Selector}; reads and writes are driven by the Netty {@code IoUringEventLoop}.
    28	 * KafkaChannel still exposes a {@code selectionKey()} accessor (and the historical NIO
    29	 * {@code Selector} consumes the returned key as a map/set identity token plus
    30	 * {@code cancel()}/{@code attach(null)}). For the io_uring path nothing outside our
    31	 * package ever derefs the returned key, but we keep the contract honest by returning a
    32	 * key whose state can be inspected without crashing: interest-ops round-trip in memory,
    33	 * readiness is always zero (no JDK selector to observe it), and the key cleanly flips
    34	 * invalid on {@link #cancel()}.
    35	 *
    36	 * <p>Not thread-safe. Each KafkaChannel owns one key, manipulated only on the Processor
    37	 * thread.
    38	 */
    39	final class NoopSelectionKey extends SelectionKey {
    40	
    41	    private int interestOps;
    42	    private boolean valid = true;
    43	
    44	    @Override
    45	    public SelectableChannel channel() {
    46	        return null;
    47	    }
    48	
    49	    @Override
    50	    public Selector selector() {
    51	        return null;
    52	    }
    53	
    54	    @Override
    55	    public boolean isValid() {
    56	        return valid;
    57	    }
    58	
    59	    @Override
    60	    public void cancel() {
    61	        valid = false;
    62	    }
    63	
    64	    @Override
    65	    public int interestOps() {
    66	        return interestOps;
    67	    }
    68	
    69	    @Override
    70	    public SelectionKey interestOps(int ops) {
    71	        this.interestOps = ops;
    72	        return this;
    73	    }
    74	
    75	    @Override
    76	    public int readyOps() {
    77	        return 0;
    78	    }
    79	}

  1547	  /**
  1548	   * Non-blocking variant of [[inc]] used by the io_uring Processor accept path.
  1549	   *
  1550	   * On the NIO path [[inc]] blocks the dedicated Acceptor thread when the broker is at
  1551	   * max.connections or when the listener connection-rate quota requires a throttle.
  1552	   * On the io_uring path the caller is the Processor's poll loop — blocking it would
  1553	   * stall every other channel on that Processor, including in-flight requests. This
  1554	   * method instead refuses (returns false) when the slot is unavailable or a throttle
  1555	   * applies, after un-recording the rate-sensor sample so accounting stays correct.
  1556	   *
  1557	   * Like [[inc]], may throw [[TooManyConnectionsException]] (per-IP max) or
  1558	   * [[ConnectionThrottledException]] (per-IP rate); callers MUST close the channel on
  1559	   * either return-false or exception.
  1560	   */
  1561	  def tryInc(listenerName: ListenerName, address: InetAddress): Boolean = {
  1562	    counts.synchronized {
  1563	      val timeMs = time.milliseconds
  1564	      val throttleTimeMs = math.max(recordConnectionAndGetThrottleTimeMs(listenerName, timeMs), 0)
  1565	      if (throttleTimeMs > 0 || !connectionSlotAvailable(listenerName)) {
  1566	        // Un-record the speculative +1 from recordConnectionAndGetThrottleTimeMs so the
  1567	        // rate sensor doesn't drift when we refuse the connection. Mirrors the un-record
  1568	        // path used by recordIpConnectionMaybeThrottle for IP throttle refusal.
  1569	        if (!protectedListener(listenerName)) {
  1570	          brokerConnectionRateSensor.record(-1.0, timeMs, false)
  1571	        }
  1572	        maxConnectionsPerListener
  1573	          .get(listenerName)
  1574	          .foreach(_.connectionRateSensor.record(-1.0, timeMs, false))
  1575	        return false
  1576	      }
  1577	
  1578	      recordIpConnectionMaybeThrottle(listenerName, address)
  1579	      val count = counts.getOrElseUpdate(address, 0)
  1580	      counts.put(address, count + 1)
  1581	      totalCount += 1
  1582	      if (listenerCounts.contains(listenerName)) {
  1583	        listenerCounts.put(listenerName, listenerCounts(listenerName) + 1)
  1584	      }
  1585	      val max = maxConnectionsPerIpOverrides.getOrElse(address, defaultMaxConnectionsPerIp)
  1586	      if (count >= max)
  1587	        throw new TooManyConnectionsException(address, max)
  1588	      true
  1589	    }
  1590	  }
  1591

  1607	  private[network] def updateBrokerMaxConnectionRate(maxConnectionRate: Int): Unit = {
  1608	    // if there is a connection waiting on the rate throttle delay, we will let it wait the original delay even if
  1609	    // the rate limit increases, because it is just one connection per listener and the code is simpler that way
  1610	    updateConnectionRateQuota(maxConnectionRate, ConnectionQuotaEntity.brokerQuotaEntity())
  1611	  }

  1613	  /**
  1614	   * Update the connection rate quota for a given IP and updates quota configs for updated IPs.
  1615	   * If an IP is given, metric config will be updated only for the given IP, otherwise
  1616	   * all metric configs will be checked and updated if required.
  1617	   *
  1618	   * @param ip ip to update or default if None
  1619	   * @param maxConnectionRate new connection rate, or resets entity to default if None
  1620	   */
  1621	  def updateIpConnectionRateQuota(ip: Option[InetAddress], maxConnectionRate: Option[Int]): Unit = synchronized {
  1622	    def isIpConnectionRateMetric(metricName: MetricName) = {
  1623	      metricName.name == ConnectionQuotaEntity.CONNECTION_RATE_METRIC_NAME &&
  1624	      metricName.group == MetricsGroup &&
  1625	      metricName.tags.containsKey(ConnectionQuotaEntity.IP_METRIC_TAG)
  1626	    }
  1627	
  1628	    def shouldUpdateQuota(metric: KafkaMetric, quotaLimit: Int) = {
  1629	      quotaLimit != metric.config.quota.bound
  1630	    }
  1631	
  1632	    ip match {
  1633	      case Some(address) =>
  1634	        // synchronize on counts to ensure reading an IP connection rate quota and creating a quota config is atomic
  1635	        counts.synchronized {
  1636	          maxConnectionRate match {
  1637	            case Some(rate) =>
  1638	              info(s"Updating max connection rate override for $address to $rate")
  1639	              connectionRatePerIp.put(address, rate)
  1640	            case None =>
  1641	              info(s"Removing max connection rate override for $address")
  1642	              connectionRatePerIp.remove(address)
  1643	          }
  1644	        }
  1645	        updateConnectionRateQuota(connectionRateForIp(address), ConnectionQuotaEntity.ipQuotaEntity(address))
  1646	      case None =>
  1647	        // synchronize on counts to ensure reading an IP connection rate quota and creating a quota config is atomic
  1648	        counts.synchronized {
  1649	          defaultConnectionRatePerIp = maxConnectionRate.getOrElse(QuotaConfig.IP_CONNECTION_RATE_DEFAULT.intValue())
  1650	        }
  1651	        info(s"Updated default max IP connection rate to $defaultConnectionRatePerIp")
  1652	        metrics.metrics.forEach { (metricName, metric) =>
  1653	          if (isIpConnectionRateMetric(metricName)) {
  1654	            val quota = connectionRateForIp(InetAddress.getByName(metricName.tags.get(ConnectionQuotaEntity.IP_METRIC_TAG)))
  1655	            if (shouldUpdateQuota(metric, quota)) {
  1656	              debug(s"Updating existing connection rate quota config for ${metricName.tags} to $quota")
  1657	              metric.config(rateQuotaMetricConfig(quota))
  1658	            }
  1659	          }
  1660	        }
  1661	    }
  1662	  }
  1663	
  1664	  // Visible for testing
  1665	  def connectionRateForIp(ip: InetAddress): Int = {
  1666	    connectionRatePerIp.getOrDefault(ip, defaultConnectionRatePerIp)
  1667	  }

  1669	  private[network] def addListener(config: KafkaConfig, listenerName: ListenerName): Unit = {
  1670	    counts.synchronized {
  1671	      if (!maxConnectionsPerListener.contains(listenerName)) {
  1672	        val newListenerQuota = new ListenerConnectionQuota(counts, listenerName)
  1673	        maxConnectionsPerListener.put(listenerName, newListenerQuota)
  1674	        listenerCounts.put(listenerName, 0)
  1675	        config.addReconfigurable(newListenerQuota)
  1676	        newListenerQuota.configure(config.valuesWithPrefixOverride(listenerName.configPrefix))
  1677	      }
  1678	      counts.notifyAll()
  1679	    }
  1680	  }
  1681	
  1682	  private[network] def removeListener(config: KafkaConfig, listenerName: ListenerName): Unit = {
  1683	    counts.synchronized {
  1684	      maxConnectionsPerListener.remove(listenerName).foreach { listenerQuota =>
  1685	        listenerCounts.remove(listenerName)
  1686	        // once listener is removed from maxConnectionsPerListener, no metrics will be recorded into listener's sensor
  1687	        // so it is safe to remove sensor here
  1688	        listenerQuota.close()
  1689	        counts.notifyAll() // wake up any waiting acceptors to close cleanly
  1690	        config.removeReconfigurable(listenerQuota)
  1691	      }
  1692	    }
  1693	  }

  1695	  def dec(listenerName: ListenerName, address: InetAddress): Unit = {
  1696	    counts.synchronized {
  1697	      val count = counts.getOrElse(address,
  1698	        throw new IllegalArgumentException(s"Attempted to decrease connection count for address with no connections, address: $address"))
  1699	      if (count == 1)
  1700	        counts.remove(address)
  1701	      else
  1702	        counts.put(address, count - 1)
  1703	
  1704	      if (totalCount <= 0)
  1705	        error(s"Attempted to decrease total connection count for broker with no connections")
  1706	      totalCount -= 1
  1707	
  1708	      if (maxConnectionsPerListener.contains(listenerName)) {
  1709	        val listenerCount = listenerCounts(listenerName)
  1710	        if (listenerCount == 0)
  1711	          error(s"Attempted to decrease connection count for listener $listenerName with no connections")
  1712	        else
  1713	          listenerCounts.put(listenerName, listenerCount - 1)
  1714	      }
  1715	      counts.notifyAll() // wake up any acceptors waiting to process a new connection since listener connection limit was reached
  1716	    }
  1717	  }
  1718	
  1719	  def get(address: InetAddress): Int = counts.synchronized {
  1720	    counts.getOrElse(address, 0)
  1721	  }

  1723	  private def waitForConnectionSlot(listenerName: ListenerName,
  1724	                                    acceptorBlockedPercentMeter: com.yammer.metrics.core.Meter): Unit = {
  1725	    counts.synchronized {
  1726	      val startThrottleTimeMs = time.milliseconds
  1727	      val throttleTimeMs = math.max(recordConnectionAndGetThrottleTimeMs(listenerName, startThrottleTimeMs), 0)
  1728	
  1729	      if (throttleTimeMs > 0 || !connectionSlotAvailable(listenerName)) {
  1730	        val startNs = time.nanoseconds
  1731	        val endThrottleTimeMs = startThrottleTimeMs + throttleTimeMs
  1732	        var remainingThrottleTimeMs = throttleTimeMs
  1733	        do {
  1734	          counts.wait(remainingThrottleTimeMs)
  1735	          remainingThrottleTimeMs = math.max(endThrottleTimeMs - time.milliseconds, 0)
  1736	        } while (remainingThrottleTimeMs > 0 || !connectionSlotAvailable(listenerName))
  1737	        acceptorBlockedPercentMeter.mark(time.nanoseconds - startNs)
  1738	      }
  1739	    }
  1740	  }
  1741	
  1742	  // This is invoked in every poll iteration and we close one LRU connection in an iteration
  1743	  // if necessary
  1744	  def maxConnectionsExceeded(listenerName: ListenerName): Boolean = {
  1745	    totalCount > brokerMaxConnections && !protectedListener(listenerName)
  1746	  }
  1747	
  1748	  private def connectionSlotAvailable(listenerName: ListenerName): Boolean = {
  1749	    if (listenerCounts(listenerName) >= maxListenerConnections(listenerName))
  1750	      false
  1751	    else if (protectedListener(listenerName))
  1752	      true
  1753	    else
  1754	      totalCount < brokerMaxConnections
  1755	  }
  1756	
  1757	  private def protectedListener(listenerName: ListenerName): Boolean =
  1758	    interBrokerListenerName == listenerName && listenerCounts.size > 1
  1759	
  1760	  private def maxListenerConnections(listenerName: ListenerName): Int =
  1761	    maxConnectionsPerListener.get(listenerName).map(_.maxConnections).getOrElse(Int.MaxValue)
  1762	
  1763	  /**
  1764	   * Calculates the delay needed to bring the observed connection creation rate to listener-level limit or to broker-wide
  1765	   * limit, whichever the longest. The delay is capped to the quota window size defined by QuotaWindowSizeSecondsProp
  1766	   *
  1767	   * @param listenerName listener for which calculate the delay
  1768	   * @param timeMs current time in milliseconds
  1769	   * @return delay in milliseconds
  1770	   */
  1771	  private def recordConnectionAndGetThrottleTimeMs(listenerName: ListenerName, timeMs: Long): Long = {
  1772	    def recordAndGetListenerThrottleTime(minThrottleTimeMs: Int): Int = {
  1773	      maxConnectionsPerListener
  1774	        .get(listenerName)
  1775	        .map { listenerQuota =>
  1776	          val listenerThrottleTimeMs = recordAndGetThrottleTimeMs(listenerQuota.connectionRateSensor, timeMs)
  1777	          val throttleTimeMs = math.max(minThrottleTimeMs, listenerThrottleTimeMs)
  1778	          // record throttle time due to hitting connection rate quota
  1779	          if (throttleTimeMs > 0) {
  1780	            listenerQuota.listenerConnectionRateThrottleSensor.record(throttleTimeMs.toDouble, timeMs)
  1781	          }
  1782	          throttleTimeMs
  1783	        }
  1784	        .getOrElse(0)
  1785	    }
  1786	
  1787	    if (protectedListener(listenerName)) {
  1788	      recordAndGetListenerThrottleTime(0)
  1789	    } else {
  1790	      val brokerThrottleTimeMs = recordAndGetThrottleTimeMs(brokerConnectionRateSensor, timeMs)
  1791	      recordAndGetListenerThrottleTime(brokerThrottleTimeMs)
  1792	    }
  1793	  }
  1794	
  1795	  /**
  1796	   * Record IP throttle time on the corresponding listener. To avoid over-recording listener/broker connection rate, we
  1797	   * also un-record the listener and broker connection if the IP gets throttled.
  1798	   *
  1799	   * @param listenerName listener to un-record connection
  1800	   * @param throttleMs IP throttle time to record for listener
  1801	   * @param timeMs current time in milliseconds
  1802	   */
  1803	  private def updateListenerMetrics(listenerName: ListenerName, throttleMs: Long, timeMs: Long): Unit = {
  1804	    if (!protectedListener(listenerName)) {
  1805	      brokerConnectionRateSensor.record(-1.0, timeMs, false)
  1806	    }
  1807	    maxConnectionsPerListener
  1808	      .get(listenerName)
  1809	      .foreach { listenerQuota =>
  1810	        listenerQuota.ipConnectionRateThrottleSensor.record(throttleMs.toDouble, timeMs)
  1811	        listenerQuota.connectionRateSensor.record(-1.0, timeMs, false)
  1812	      }
  1813	  }
